# Rigveda Samhita — Volume 22 (Kannada → English translation)

### Printed p. 1

श्रीः (*śrīḥ*)

श्री गणाधिपतये नमः (*śrī gaṇādhipataye namaḥ*)

वागीशाद्याः सुमनसः सर्वार्थानामुपक्रमे । यं नत्वा कृतकृत्याः स्युस्तं नमामि गजाननं ॥ (*vāgīśādyāḥ sumanasaḥ sarvārthānāmupakrame | yaṃ natvā kṛtakṛtyāḥ syustaṃ namāmi gajānanaṃ ‖*) **GLOSS**

यस्य निःश्वसितं वेदा यो वेदेभ्योऽखिलं जगत् । निर्ममे तमहं वंदे विद्यातीर्थमहेश्वरं ॥ (*yasya niḥśvasitaṃ vedā yo vedebhyo 'khilaṃ jagat | nirmame tamahaṃ vaṃde vidyātīrthamaheśvaraṃ ‖*) **GLOSS**

वेदः शिवः शिवो वेदो वेदाध्यायी सदाशिवः । तस्मात्सर्वप्रयत्नेन वेदमेव सदा जपेत् ॥ (*vedaḥ śivaḥ śivo vedo vedādhyāyī sadāśivaḥ | tasmātsarvaprayatnena vedameva sadā japet ‖*) **GLOSS**

**Ṛgveda Saṃhitā with the Bhāṣya of Sāyaṇa (with the Kannada translation and explanation)**

**Part 22**

**Fifth Aṣṭaka, Third Adhyāya**

**Sūktas 20–31 of the Seventh Maṇḍala**

**Introduction**

Of the seventh maṇḍala, the fourteen sūktas from the 18th to the 31st are all addressed to Indra. Of these, sūktas 18 and 19 are included in the preceding adhyāya, that is, in Ṛ. Saṃ. Part 21. Therefore the twelve sūktas from 20 to 31 in this part are in praise of Indra. Since we have described Indra at length in Ṛ. Saṃ. Part 5, pp. 569-582, and Part 16, pp. 631-683, and elsewhere, from here onwards particular matters will be described only where the need appears.

## Sūkta 20 (Maṇḍala 7)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अथ तृतीयोऽध्याय आरभ्यते ॥ उग्रो जज्ञे इति दशर्चं तृतीयं सूक्तं
> वसिष्ठस्यार्षं त्रैष्टुभमैंद्रं । तथा चानुक्रम्यते । उग्रो दशेति ॥ आभिप्लविके च

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Now the third adhyāya begins. The sūkta उग्रो जज्ञे (*ugro jajñe*) of ten Ṛks is the third sūkta; its seer is Vasiṣṭha, its metre is Triṣṭubh and its deity is Indra. So the Anukramaṇī says: उग्रो दश (*ugro daśa*). In the Ābhiplavika (the sentence continues in the next block).

### Printed p. 2

> तुर्थेऽहनि निष्केवल्य एतत्सूक्तं निविद्धानं । सूत्रितं च । चतुर्थस्योग्रो जज्ञे
> इति निष्केवल्यं । आ. ७-२ । इति ॥ महाव्रतेऽपि निष्केवल्य एतत्सूक्तं । तथैव पंचमारण्यके सूत्रितं । उग्रो जज्ञे वीर्याय स्वधावानुदु ब्रह्माण्यैरत श्रवस्या । ऐ.
> आ. ५-२-९ । इति ॥ सौमिकचातुर्मास्येषु वैश्वदेवस्य स्थाने प्रथमं पृष्ठ्यमहः ।
> तत्रापि निष्केवल्य एतत्सूक्तं निविद्धानं । सूत्रितं च । जनिष्ठा उग्र उग्रो जज्ञे इति
> मध्यंदिनः । आ. ९-२ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

On the fourth day this sūkta is the nividdhāna in the niṣkevalya recitation. It is prescribed in the sūtra चतुर्थस्योग्रो जज्ञे इति निष्केवल्यं (*caturthasyogro jajñe iti niṣkevalyaṃ*) (Ā. 7-2). In the Mahāvrata too this sūkta belongs to the niṣkevalya, and it is likewise prescribed in the fifth Āraṇyaka: उग्रो जज्ञे वीर्याय स्वधावानुदु ब्रह्माण्यैरत श्रवस्या (*ugro jajñe vīryāya svadhāvānudu brahmāṇyairata śravasyā*) (Ai. Ā. 5-2-9). In the Soma-sacrifices called Cāturmāsya, in the place of the Vaiśvadeva, comes the first Pṛṣṭhya day; there too this sūkta is the nividdhāna in the niṣkevalya, and it is prescribed in the sūtra जनिष्ठा उग्र उग्रो जज्ञे इति मध्यंदिनः (*janiṣṭhā ugra ugro jajñe iti madhyaṃdinaḥ*) (Ā. 9-2).

Translation: Since the explanation of the second adhyāya of the fifth aṣṭaka was completed in the previous part, the explanation of the third adhyāya is now begun. This sūkta beginning उग्रो जज्ञे (*ugro jajñe*) is the third sūkta in the second anuvāka of the seventh maṇḍala. It contains ten Ṛks. Vasiṣṭha is the seer of this sūkta; Indra is the deity; the metre is Triṣṭubh. In the Anukramaṇī it is stated as उग्रो दश (*ugro daśa*). In the sacrifices connected with the Abhiplava, when the niṣkevalya śastra mantras are recited on the fourth day, this sūkta is to be recited as the nividdhāna sūkta; this is set forth by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra चतुर्थस्योग्रो जज्ञे इति निष्केवल्यं (*caturthasyogro jajñe iti niṣkevalyaṃ*) (Ā. 7-2).

In the Mahāvrata rite too, this sūkta is to be recited among the niṣkevalya śastra mantras; this is indicated in the Aitareya Āraṇyaka, in the fifth Āraṇyaka, by the sentence उग्रो जज्ञे वीर्याय स्वधावानुदु ब्रह्माण्यैरत श्रवस्या (*ugro jajñe vīryāya svadhāvānudu brahmāṇyairata śravasyā*) (Ai. Ā. 5-2-9). And in the Soma-sacrifices called Cāturmāsya, when the work of the first day of the sacrifice called Pṛṣṭhya is performed in the place of the Vaiśvadeva, this sūkta is to be recited as the nividdhāna sūkta in the middle of the niṣkevalya śastra mantras; this is set forth by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra जनिष्ठा उग्र उग्रो जज्ञे इति मध्यंदिनः (*janiṣṭhā ugra ugro jajñe iti madhyaṃdinaḥ*) (Ā. 9-2).

Sūkta 20. Maṇḍala 7; Anuvāka 2; Sūkta 20. Aṣṭaka 5; Adhyāya 3; Varga 1, 2. Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 10. Seer: Vasiṣṭha. Deity: Indra. Metre: Triṣṭubh.

#### Ṛk 1

**NOTE**

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उ॒ग्रो ज॑ज्ञे वी॒र्या॑य स्व॒धावा॒ञ्चक्रि॒रपो॒ नर्यो॒ यत्क॑रि॒ष्यन् ।

### Printed p. 3

> जग्मिर्युवा नृषदनमवोभिस्त्राता न इंद्र एनसो महश्चित् ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उग्रः । जज्ञे । वीर्याय । स्वधावान् । चक्रिः । अपः । नर्यः । यत् । करिष्यन् ।  
> जग्मिः । युवा । नृसदनं । अवःभिः । त्राता । नः । इंद्रः । एनसः । महः । चित् ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> स्वधावान् बलवानुग्र ओजस्व्यद्ग्रोर्वेंद्रो वीर्याय वीर्यं कर्तुं जज्ञे । बभूव । नर्यो नरहितः सन् यत्कर्म करिष्यन् भवति तदपः कर्म चक्रिः कर्तैव ॥ चक्रिरिति किन्प्रत्ययस्य लिड्वद्भावान्न लोकाव्ययनिष्ठाखलर्थतृनामिति षष्ठीप्रतिषेधः ॥ अपि च नृषदनं यज्ञगृहं युवा नित्यतरुणः सन् अवोभी रक्षणैः सार्धं जग्मिर्गंता महश्चिन्महतोऽप्येनसः पापान्नोऽस्माकं त्राता रक्षिता च भवेति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Svadhāvān means mighty. The fierce one, the vigorous Indra, was born (became) for heroism, to perform heroic deed. Being beneficial to men, whatever deed he intends to do, that deed he is indeed the doer of. **NOTE** Moreover, ever young, he goes to the house of the sacrifice (the place of men's sitting) together with his protections; may he be our protector, even from great sin.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- स्वधावान् (*svadhāvān*)—mighty
- उग्रः (*ugraḥ*)—of marvellous prowess
- इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—Indra
- वीर्याय (*vīryāya*)—to perform heroic deeds
- जज्ञे (*jajñe*)—has been born
- नर्यः (*naryaḥ*)—beneficial to men
- यत् (*yat*)—whichever deed
- करिष्यन् (*kariṣyan*)—he resolves to do
- अपः (*apaḥ*)—that deed
- चक्रिः (*cakriḥ*)—he surely accomplishes
- युवा (*yuvā*)—Indra, ever young
- अवोभिः (*avobhiḥ*)—with his protections
- नृषदनं (*nṛṣadanaṃ*)—the house of the sacrifice
- जग्मिः (*jagmiḥ*)—having entered
- महश्चित् (*mahaścit*)—exceedingly great
- एनसः (*enasaḥ*)—from sin (releasing us)
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- त्राता (*trātā*)—is the protector

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The mighty Indra, of marvellous prowess, has been born to perform heroic deeds. Whatever deed he resolves to do for the benefit of men, that deed he surely accomplishes. Ever young, Indra enters the house of the sacrifice with his protections and, releasing us from the gravest sin, protects us.

##### English translation
The fierce and powerful (Indra) has been born for heroic (deeds); friendly to man he is the accomplisher of whatever act he undertakes to perform; ever youthful, he invests the (Sacrificial) hall with defences (against interruption); be our preserver, Indra, from heinous sin.

### Printed p. 4

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> हंता॑ वृ॒त्रमिंद्रः॒ शूशु॑वानः॒ प्रावी॒न्नु वी॒रो ज॑रि॒तार॑मू॒ती ।  
> कर्ता॑ सु॒दासे॒ अह॒ वा उ॑ लो॒कं दाता॒ वसु॒ मुहु॒रा दा॒शुषे॑ भूत् ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> हंता । वृत्रं । इंद्रः । शूशुवानः । प्र । आवीत् । नु । वीरः । जरितारं । ऊती ।  
> कर्ता । सुदासे । अह । वै । ऊं इति । लोकं । दाता । वसु । मुहुः । आ । दाशुषे । भूत् ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> इंद्रः शूशुवानो वर्धमानः सन् वृत्रमसुरं हंता भवति ॥ तृनंतॆत्सादत्र षष्ठ्यभावः ॥ वीरो वीरः सन् जरितारं स्तोतारं नु क्षिप्रमूती ऊत्या रक्षयो प्रावीत् । प्रारक्षच्च । सुदासे राज्ञे लोकं जनपदं कर्ता च । यद्वा । सुदासे कल्याणदानाय यजमानाय लोकं कर्ता च भवति ॥ इहाप्युत्तरत्रापि तृनंतॆत्सात्षष्ठ्यभावः । अह वा उ इति त्रयः पूरणाः ॥ दाशुषे यजमानाय वसु धनं मुहुर्भूयो भूयो दाता च भूत् । अभूत् । आ इति चार्थे ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Indra, growing in strength, becomes the slayer of Vṛtra, the asura. As a hero he has promptly protected the praiser with his protections, and protected him again. He becomes the maker of a world, a country, for King Sudās; or else, he becomes the maker of a world for the sacrificer Sudās, of good gifts. Here and in what follows too the genitive does not apply. The three words अह वा उ (*aha vā u*) are expletives. He has also been, again and again, the giver of wealth to the offerer, the sacrificer. The word आ (*ā*) is in the sense of "and".

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—Indra
- शूशुवानः (*śūśuvānaḥ*)—growing in strength
- वृत्रं (*vṛtraṃ*)—the asura
- हंता (*haṃtā*)—becomes the slayer
- वीरः (*vīraḥ*)—the valiant Indra
- जरितारं (*jaritāraṃ*)—the praiser
- नु (*nu*)—promptly
- ऊती (*ūtī*)—with protections
- प्रावीत् (*prāvīt*)—has protected
- सुदासे (*sudāse*)—to the king named Sudās
- लोकं (*lokaṃ*)—the kingdom
- कर्ता (*kartā*)—becomes the provider
- अह वा उ (*aha vā u*)—moreover
- दाशुषे (*dāśuṣe*)—to the sacrificer who offers oblations
- वसु (*vasu*)—wealth
- मुहुः (*muhuḥ*)—many times
- दाता भूत् (*dātā bhūt*)—provides

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Indra, growing in strength, becomes the slayer of Vṛtra. The valiant Indra has protected the praiser with his protections. He provides the king named Sudās with a kingdom. He gives abundant gifts of wealth, many times, to the sacrificer who offers oblations.

### Printed p. 5

##### English translation
Indra, dilating in bulk, is the slayer of Vritra: the hero defends his worshipper promptly with his protection, whether he be the giver of dominion to Sudas, or the donor repeatedly of wealth to the offerer (of oblation).

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यु॒ध्मो अ॑न॒र्वा ख॑ज॒कृत्स॒मद्वा॒ शूरः॑ सत्रा॒षाट् ज॒नुषे॒मुषा॑ळ्हः ।  
> व्या॑स॒ इंद्रः॒ पृत॑नाः॒ स्वोजा॒ अधा॒ विश्वं॑ शत्रू॒यंतं॑ जघान ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> युध्मः । अनर्वा । खजकृत् । समत्ऽवा । शूरः । सत्राषाट् । जनुषा । ईं । अषाळ्हः ।  
> वि । आसे । इंद्रः । पृतनाः । सुऽओजाः । अध । विश्वं । शत्रुऽयंतं । जघान ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> युध्मो योद्धानर्वाभिगंत्ररहितो युद्धेष्वपराङ्मुखो वा खजकृद्युद्धकृत् । खले खज इति युद्धनामसु पाठात् । समद्वा । समत् कलहः । तद्वान् शूरः शौर्योपेतो जनुषा जन्मना स्वभावत एव सत्राषाट् बहूनामभिभविताषाळ्हः स्वयं च केनाप्यनभिभूतः स्वोजाः सुबल ईमयमिंद्रः पृतनाः शत्रूणां सेना व्यासे । विक्षिपति । अधापि च शत्रूयंतं शात्रवमाचरंतं विश्वं सर्वं जघान हंति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Yudhma is a warrior; anarvā is one without an opponent who approaches him, or one not turned away in battles; khajakṛt is a maker of battle, since khaja is read among the names of battle. Samadvā is the possessor of samat, strife. He is a hero, endowed with heroism, by birth, by nature, the overcomer of many; unvanquished, himself overcome by none; svojā, of good strength. This Indra scatters the armies, the hosts of the enemies. And moreover he slays everyone, all who act as enemies.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- युध्मः (*yudhmaḥ*)—the excellent warrior
- अनर्वा (*anarvā*)—who never turns away
- खजकृत् (*khajakṛt*)—skilled in battle
- समद्वा (*samadvā*)—fond of battle
- शूरः (*śūraḥ*)—the hero
- जनुषा (*januṣā*)—from birth
- सत्राषाट् (*satrāṣāṭ*)—the vanquisher of many
- अषाळ्हः (*aṣāḷhaḥ*)—the unobstructed
- स्वोजाः (*svojāḥ*)—of excellent strength
- ईं इंद्रः (*īṃ iṃdraḥ*)—this same Indra
- पृतनाः (*pṛtanāḥ*)—the hostile armies
- वि आसे (*vi āse*)—scatters
- अध (*adha*)—and
- शत्रूयंतं (*śatrūyaṃtaṃ*)—who acts as an enemy against him
- विश्वं (*viśvaṃ*)—all
- जघान (*jaghāna*)—destroys

### Printed p. 6

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

This same Indra, the excellent warrior, who never turns away, skilled in battle, fond of battle, a hero, the vanquisher of many from birth, the unobstructed, of excellent strength, scatters the hostile armies. And he destroys all the enemies who act against him.

##### English translation
A warrior who turns not back in battle, a combatant, one engaged in tumults, a hero, victorious over (his) foes from birth, invincible, of great vigor this Indra, scatters (hostile) hosts and slays all (his) adversaries.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उ॒भे चि॑दिंद्र॒ रोद॑सी महि॒त्वा प॑प्राथ॒ तवि॑षीभिस्तुविष्मः ।  
> नि वज्र॒मिंद्रो॒ हरि॑वा॒न्मिमि॑क्ष॒न्त्समंध॑सा॒ मदे॑षु॒ वा उ॑वोच ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उभे इति । चित् । इंद्र । रोदसी इति । महित्वा । आ । पप्राथ । तविषीभिः । तुविष्मः ।  
> नि । वज्रं । इंद्रः । हरिवान् । मिमिक्षन् । सं । अंधसा । मदेषु । वै । उवोच ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे तुविष्मो बहुधनेंद्र महित्वा महत्त्वेन तविषीभिर्बलैस्त्वोभे चिदुभे अपि रोदसी द्यावापृथिव्यौ आ पप्राथ । आपूरितवानसि । अथ परोक्षस्तुतिः । हरिवानश्ववानिंद्रो वज्रं नि मिमिक्षन् शत्रुषु प्रसारयन् मदेषु यज्ञेषु निविष्टे वांधसा सोमेन समुवोच । संसेव्यते संगच्छते वा ॥ उच समवाये इति धातुः । वा इति पूरणः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Tuviṣma, Indra of much wealth, by your greatness and your strengths you have filled both heaven and earth. Then follows praise in the third person: Indra, owner of bay horses, thrusting the vajra forth upon the enemies, in the joys, that is, in the sacrifices where he is seated, is joined with the Soma-food. The root uca is in the sense of union; the word वा (*vā*) is an expletive.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- तुविष्मः (*tuviṣmaḥ*)—possessor of exceeding wealth
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- महित्वा (*mahitvā*)—possessed of greatness
- तविषीभिः (*taviṣībhiḥ*)—by your strengths
- उभे चित् रोदसी (*ubhe cit rodasī*)—both heaven and earth
- आ पप्राथ (*ā paprātha*)—you have pervaded
- हरिवान् (*harivān*)—the possessor of horses
- इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—Indra
- वज्र (*vajra*)—the thunderbolt
- नि मिमिक्षन् (*ni mimikṣan*)—hurling upon the enemies
- मदेषु (*madeṣu*)—in the joy-giving sacrifices
- अंधसा (*aṃdhasā*)—with the food in the form of Soma
- सं उवोच वै (*saṃ uvoca vai*)—is united

### Printed p. 7

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, possessor of exceeding wealth, with your strengths, endowed with greatness, you have pervaded both heaven and earth. Indra, the possessor of horses, hurling the thunderbolt upon the enemies, is united with the food in the form of Soma in the joy-giving sacrifices.

##### English translation
Opulent Indra, you have filled both heaven and earth with your magnitude, your energies: Indra, the lord of horses, brandishing the thunderbolt, is gratified at sacrifices by the (sacrificial) food.

#### Ṛk 5

**NOTE**

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> वृषा॑ जजान॒ वृष॑णं॒ रणा॑य॒ तमु॑ चि॒न्नारी॒ नर्यं॑ ससूव ।  
> प्र यः से॑ना॒नीरध॒ नृभ्यो॒ अस्ती॒नः सत्वा॑ ग॒वेष॑णः॒ स धृ॒ष्णुः ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> वृषा । जजान । वृषणं । रणाय । तं । ऊं इति । चित् । नारी । नर्यं । ससूव ।  
> प्र । यः । सेनानीः । अध । नृभ्यः । अस्ति । इनः । सत्वा । गोऽएषणः । सः । धृष्णुः ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वृषा सेक्ता पिता कश्यपो वृषणं कामानां वर्षितारमिंद्रं रणाय युद्धार्थं जजान ॥ जन जनन इति धातुः ॥ नर्यं नरहितं तमु तमेवेंद्रं नारी चिददितिरपि ससूव । सुषुवे । अथापि च य इंद्रो नृभ्यो नृणां सेनानीः सेनानां नेता सन् प्रास्ति प्रभवति स इंद्र इनः सर्वस्य जगत ईश्वरो भवति । नियुत्वानिन इतीश्वरनामसु पाठात् । सत्वा शत्रूणां सादकश्च गवेषणो गवामन्वेष्टा च धृष्णुः शत्रूणां धर्षकश्च भवतीति शेषः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The showerer (vṛṣā), the sprinkler, that is, the father Kaśyapa, begot Indra, the showerer of desires, for battle, for the sake of war. That same Indra, beneficial to men, the woman Aditi also bore. Moreover, the Indra who, as leader of the armies of men, prevails, that Indra is the lord (ina) of the whole world, since नियुत्वानिन (*niyutvānina*) is read among the names of lord. He is the destroyer of enemies, the seeker of cows, and the subduer of enemies; "he is" is to be supplied.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वृषा (*vṛṣā*)—the father Kaśyapa
- वृषणं (*vṛṣaṇaṃ*)—Indra, the showerer of desires
- रणाय (*raṇāya*)—for the sake of battle
- जजान (*jajāna*)—begot
- नर्यं (*naryaṃ*)—beneficial to men
- तमु (*tamu*)—that same Indra
- नारी चित् (*nārī cit*)—his mother Aditi too
- ससूव (*sasūva*)—bore
- अध (*adha*)—afterwards
- यः (*yaḥ*)—which Indra
- नृभ्यः सेनानीः (*nṛbhyaḥ senānīḥ*)—as leader of all men
- प्र अस्ति (*pra asti*)—exerts the influence of a leader

### Printed p. 8

- सः (*saḥ*)—that same Indra
- इनः (*inaḥ*)—is the lord of the whole world
- सत्वा (*satvā*)—victorious
- गवेषणः (*gaveṣaṇaḥ*)—the one who seeks out and brings the cows
- धृष्णुः (*dhṛṣṇuḥ*)—the subduer of enemies

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The father Kaśyapa begot Indra, the showerer of desires, for the very purpose of battle. That same Indra, beneficial to men, his mother Aditi also bore. Afterwards, the Indra who exerts his influence as the leader of the whole army of men, that same Indra is the lord of the whole world. He is victorious, the one who seeks out and brings the cows, and the subduer of enemies.

##### English translation
(His) progenitor begot Indra, the showerer (of benefits) for (the purpose of) war : his mother brought him forth the benefactor of man : the leader of armies who is chief over men, he is the lord, the conqueror, the recoverer of the cows, the subduer of foes.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> नू चि॒त्स भ्रे॑षते॒ जनो॒ न रे॑ष॒न्मनो॒ यो अ॑स्य घो॒रमा॒विवा॑सात् ।  
> य॒ज्ञैर्य इंद्रे॒ दध॑ते॒ दुवां॑सि॒ क्षय॒त्स रा॒य ऋ॑त॒पा ऋ॑ते॒जाः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> नु । चित् । सः । भ्रेषते । जनः । न । रेषत् । मनः । यः । अस्य । घोरं । आविवासात् ।  
> यज्ञैः । यः । इंद्रे । दधते । दुवांसि । क्षयत् । सः । राये । ऋतपाः । ऋतेजाः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यो जनोऽस्येंद्रस्य घोरं शत्रूणां बाधकं मनो यज्ञैराविवासात् परिचरति स जनः । नु इति प्रतिषेधे वर्तते । चिदित्येवकारार्थे । नू चित् नैव भ्रेषते । स्थानात् न भ्रश्यति । न रेषत् । नैव क्षीयेत । अपि च यो जनो दुवांसि परिचरणसाधनानि स्तोत्रशस्त्राणींद्रे दधते निधत्ते तस्मै जनाय ऋतपा यज्ञपाता ऋतेजा यज्ञे जातश्च स इंद्रो राये धनाय क्षयत् । निवसति । भवेदित्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The man who serves with sacrifices the terrible mind of this Indra, the mind that obstructs enemies, that man never falls from his place and never perishes; नु (*nu*) is used in the sense of prohibition, and चित् (*cit*) in the sense of "indeed". Moreover, to the man who places in Indra the means of service, hymns and recitations, that Indra, the protector of the sacrifice (ṛtapā) and born in the sacrifice (ṛtejā), dwells for wealth; that is, he would be there to give it.

### Printed p. 9

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यः (*yaḥ*)—the man who
- अस्य (*asya*)—of this Indra
- घोरं (*ghoraṃ*)—terrible to enemies
- मनः (*manaḥ*)—the mind
- यज्ञैः (*yajñaiḥ*)—with sacrificial rites
- आविवासात् (*āvivāsāt*)—serves
- सः जनः (*saḥ janaḥ*)—that same man
- नू चित् भ्रेषते (*nū cit bhreṣate*)—surely does not fall from his position
- न रेषत् (*na reṣat*)—suffers no loss
- यः (*yaḥ*)—the man who
- दुवांसि (*duvāṃsi*)—hymns, recitations and the like, the means of service
- इंद्रे (*iṃdre*)—in Indra
- दधते (*dadhate*)—offers; to such a man
- ऋतपाः (*ṛtapāḥ*)—the protector of the sacrifice
- ऋतेजाः (*ṛtejāḥ*)—born in the sacrifice
- सः (*saḥ*)—that same Indra
- राये (*rāye*)—for wealth (to give wealth)
- क्षयत् (*kṣayat*)—dwells with him

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The man who serves with sacrificial rites the mind of Indra, terrible to enemies, that same man surely does not fall from his position. To the man who offers to Indra the hymns, recitations and mantras that are the means of service, that same Indra, who is the protector of the sacrifice and born in the sacrifice, remains with him alone to give wealth.

##### English translation
He who d votes his mind to the terrible Indra never falls (from his condition), nor will he perish : the protector of sacred rites, the progeny of sacrifice bestows riches on him who offers to Indra praises and prayers with sacrifices.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यदिं॑द्र॒ पूर्वो॒ अप॑राय॒ शिक्ष॒न्नय॒ज्ज्याया॒न्कनी॑यसो दे॒ष्णं ।  
> अ॒मृत॒ इत्पर्या॑सीत दू॒रमा चि॑त्र॒ चित्र्यं॑ भरा र॒यिं नः॑ ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यत् । इंद्र । पूर्वः । अपराय । शिक्षन् । अयत् । ज्यायान् । कनीयसः । देष्णं ।  
> अमृतः । इत् । परि । आसीत । दूरं । आ । चित्र । चित्र्यं । भर । रयिं । नः ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे चित्र चायनीयेंद्र यद्धनं पूर्वः पिता ज्येष्ठो भ्राता वापराय पुत्राय कनीयसे वा शिक्षन् प्रयच्छन् । शिक्षतिर्दानकर्मा प्रीणाति शिक्षतीति दानकर्म
> 2

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O wonderful Indra (citra, to be gladly invoked), whatever wealth the first, the father or the elder brother, giving (śikṣan) to the later, the son or the younger brother, since शिक्षतिर्दानकर्मा (*śikṣatirdānakarmā*), the verb śikṣ is read among words of giving; "becomes" is to be supplied.

### Printed p. 10

> सु पाठात् । भवतीति शेषः । यच्च देष्णं देयं धनं ज्यायान् ज्येष्ठः कनीयसोऽयत् प्राप्नुयात् । यच्चापि धनं पितृतो लब्ध्वा पुत्रोऽमृत इत् अमृत एव सन् पितृगृहं विहाय दूरं पर्यासीत आस्ते तत् त्रिविधं चित्र्यं चायनीयं रयिं धनं नोऽस्मभ्यमा भर । आहर ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

And whatever wealth to be given (deṣṇa) the elder obtains from the younger, and whatever wealth a son, having received it from his father, living on, leaves his father's house and dwells far away: that threefold wonderful, desirable wealth, bring to us.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- चित्र (*citra*)—of attractive form
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- यत् (*yat*)—whichever wealth
- पूर्वः (*pūrvaḥ*)—the father, first born
- अपराय (*aparāya*)—to the son born of his own body
- शिक्षन् (*śikṣan*)—gives
- देष्णं (*deṣṇaṃ*)—whichever wealth is rightly to be given
- ज्यायान् (*jyāyān*)—the elder
- कनीयसः (*kanīyasaḥ*)—to the younger
- अयत् (*ayat*)—causes to obtain
- यत् (*yat*)—(after obtaining) whichever wealth
- अमृत इत् (*amṛta it*)—while still living, the son
- दूरं (*dūraṃ*)—separated from his father
- पर्यासीत (*paryāsīta*)—dwells independently
- चित्र्यं (*citryaṃ*)—attractive
- रयिं (*rayiṃ*)—all that wealth
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- आ भर (*ā bhara*)—bring and grant

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra of attractive form, whatever wealth the first-born father gives to the son born of his own body, whatever wealth rightly to be given the elder causes the younger to obtain, and whatever wealth, once obtained, the son, while still living, dwells independently, separated from his father, all that attractive wealth bring and grant to us.

##### English translation
That (wealth) Indra, whieh the prior has given to the posterior: which the elder may accept from the younger: with which (the son) yet liveing dwells far away (separated from his father), confer, wonderful Indra, such precious riches upon us.

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यस्त॑ इंद्र प्रि॒यो जनो॒ ददा॑श॒दस॑न्निरे॒के अ॑द्रिवः॒ सखा॑ ते ।  
> व॒यं ते॑ अ॒स्यां सु॑म॒तौ चनि॑ष्ठाः॒ स्याम॒ वरू॑थे॒ अघ्न॑तो॒ नृपी॑तौ ॥

### Printed p. 11

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यः । ते । इंद्र । प्रियः । जनः । ददाशत् । असत् । निरेके । अद्रिवः । सखा । ते ।  
> वयं । ते । अस्यां । सुमतौ । चनिष्ठाः । स्याम । वरूथे । अघ्नतः । नृपीतौ ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र यस्ते तुभ्यं प्रियः सखा जनो ददाशत् हवींषि दद्यात् हे अद्रिवः स सखा ते तव निरेके दानेऽसत् । स्यात् । वयं च वसिष्ठा अघ्नतोऽहिंसतस्ते तवास्यां सुमतावनुग्रहबुद्धौ वर्तमानाश्चनिष्ठाः स्तुतिमत्तरा अतिशयेनान्नवंतोवा । चनोऽन्नं । नृपीतौ नृणां रक्षके वरूथे गृहे वरणीये वा धने स्याम । वसेम भवेम वा ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, the dear friend, the man who gives you oblations, O wielder of the stone (adrivaḥ), may that friend be in your giving (nireka). And we, the Vasiṣṭhas, abiding in this favourable disposition, this favour of you who do not injure, may be most praising, or exceedingly possessed of food (cana is food); and in the house that protects men (nṛpīti), or in desirable wealth, may we dwell or may we be.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अद्रिवः (*adrivaḥ*)—O thunderbolt-bearer
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- यः (*yaḥ*)—the man who
- ते (*te*)—to you
- प्रियः जनः (*priyaḥ janaḥ*)—as a beloved friend
- ददाशत् (*dadāśat*)—offers oblations
- सखा (*sakhā*)—that same friend of yours
- ते (*te*)—your
- निरेके (*nireke*)—as one fit for your gift
- असत् (*asat*)—may he be
- वयं (*vayaṃ*)—we too, born in the line of Vasiṣṭha
- अघ्नतः (*aghnataḥ*)—of you who are free from injury
- ते (*te*)—your
- अस्यां सुमतौ (*asyāṃ sumatau*)—abiding in this favourable disposition
- चनिष्ठाः (*caniṣṭhāḥ*)—possessed of abundant food
- नृपीतौ (*nṛpītau*)—protecting men
- वरूथे (*varūthe*)—in the house
- स्याम (*syāma*)—may we be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, bearer of the thunderbolt, the man who offers oblations to you as a beloved friend, may that same friend of yours be fit for your gift. May we too, born in the line of Vasiṣṭha, abiding in the favourable disposition of you who are free from injury, be possessed of abundant food and dwell in the house that protects men.

##### English translation
May the man who is dear to you, Indra, present (oblations) : may he be your friend, weilder of the thunderbolt he(assiduous)in donations: may we be abounding in food through this favour of you who are devoid of cruelty, (may we be in the enjoyment of) a dwelling giving shelter to men.

#### Ṛk 9

**NOTE**

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ए॒ष स्तोमो॑ अचिक्रद॒द्वृषा॑ त उ॒त स्ता॒मुर्म॑घवन्नक्रपिष्ट ।  
> रा॒यस्कामो॑ जरि॒तारं॑ त॒ आग॒न्त्वमं॒ग श॑क्र॒ वस्व॒ आ श॑को नः ॥

### Printed p. 12

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> एषः । स्तोमः । अचिक्रदत् । वृषा । ते । उत । स्तामुः । मघवन् । अक्रपिष्ट ।  
> रायः । कामः । जरितारं । ते । आ । अगन् । त्वं । अंग । शक्र । वस्वः । आ । शकः । नः ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे मघवन् धनवन्निंद्र ते त्वदर्थं वृषा सेक्त्रैष स्तोमः सोमः सूयमानोऽचिक्रदत् । क्रंदति । उतापि च स्तामुः स्तोताक्रपिष्ट । अस्तौत् । अपि च हे शक्र ते तव जरितारं स्तोतारं मां रायो धनस्य कामोऽभिलाष आगन् । आगतः । अतस्त्वं वस्वो धनं ॥ कर्मणि षष्ठी ॥ नोऽस्मभ्यमंग क्षिप्रमा शकः । धेहि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Maghavan, wealthy Indra, for you this showering stoma, that is, Soma being pressed, cries out; **NOTE** and the praiser (stotā) has also praised. And, O Śakra, to me, your praiser, desire for wealth has come. Therefore, bestow upon us quickly (aṅga) wealth.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मघवन् (*maghavan*)—O wealthy Indra
- ते (*te*)—addressed to you
- वृषा (*vṛṣā*)—showering of desires
- एषः स्तोमः (*eṣaḥ stomaḥ*)—this hymn
- अचिक्रदत् (*acikradat*)—is being recited
- उत (*uta*)—and
- स्तामुः (*stāmuḥ*)—the praiser too
- अक्रपिष्ट (*akrapiṣṭa*)—has praised
- शक्र (*śakra*)—O Indra
- ते (*te*)—your
- जरितारं (*jaritāraṃ*)—me, the praiser
- रायः (*rāyaḥ*)—of wealth
- कामः (*kāmaḥ*)—the desire
- अगन् (*agan*)—has pervaded
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- वस्वः (*vasvaḥ*)—wealth
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- अंग (*aṃga*)—quickly
- आ शकः (*ā śakaḥ*)—provide

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O wealthy Indra, this hymn, showering desires, is being recited addressed to you. And the praiser too has praised. O Indra, desire for wealth has pervaded me, your praiser. Provide us quickly with wealth.

##### English translation
For you, Maghavan, this showering soma ( libation ) cries aloud: to you the worshipper has recited praises : the desire of riches has fallen upon your adorer, therfore Shakra, bestow quickly upon us wealth.

#### Ṛk 10

**NOTE**

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स न॑ इंद्र॒ त्वय॑ताया इ॒षे धा॒स्त्मना॑ च॒ ये म॒घवा॑नो जु॒नंति॑ ।  
> वस्वे॒ षु ते॑ जरि॒त्रे अ॑स्तु श॒क्तिर्यू॒यं पा॑त स्व॒स्तिभिः॒ सदा॑ नः ॥

### Printed p. 13

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सः । नः । इंद्र । त्वयतायै । इषे । धाः । त्मना । च । ये । मघवानः । जुनंति ।  
> वस्वी । सु । ते । जरित्रे । अस्तु । शक्तिः । यूयं । पात । स्वस्तिभिः । सदा । नः ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र स त्वं त्वयतायै इषे त्वया दत्तमन्नं भोक्तुं नोऽस्मान् धाः । धारय । ये च मघवानो हविष्मंतस्त्मना स्वयमेव जुनंति हवींषि त्वां प्रति प्रेरयंति तानपि त्वयतायै इषे धाः । अपि च वस्वीष्वत्यंतं प्रशस्तासु स्तुतिषु ते तव जरित्रे स्तोत्रे मह्यं शक्तिः सामर्थ्यमस्तु । यद्वा । जरित्रे मह्यं ते तव वस्वीषु प्रशस्ता शक्तिर्दानमस्तु । स्पष्टमन्यत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, you, for the food given by you (त्वयतायै इषे (*tvayatāyai iṣe*)), enable us to partake of it. And those wealthy ones, the possessors of oblations, who of their own accord send oblations towards you, them too establish for that food. Moreover, in the exceedingly excellent praises, may there be power, ability, for me, your praiser. Or else: may there be for me, the praiser, your excellent gift in the matter of praises. The rest is clear.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- सः (*saḥ*)—you
- धाः (*dhāḥ*)—make us capable
- ये च (*ye ca*)—and whoever
- मघवानः (*maghavānaḥ*)—being possessed of oblations
- त्मना (*tmanā*)—by their own impulse
- जुनंति (*junaṃti*)—offer oblations to you (make them also fit for that food)
- वस्वी षु (*vasvī ṣu*)—with exceedingly excellent praises
- ते (*te*)—your
- जरित्रे (*jaritre*)—to me, the praiser
- शक्तिः (*śaktiḥ*)—ability
- अस्तु (*astu*)—let there be
- यूयं (*yūyaṃ*)—you, O Indra and the other gods
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- सदा (*sadā*)—always
- स्वस्तिभिः (*svastibhiḥ*)—with blessings
- पात (*pāta*)—protect

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, make us capable of enjoying the food given by you. Those who, possessed of oblations, offer oblations to you of their own impulse, make them also fit for that food. May I, who praise you with exceedingly excellent praises, have ability. O Indra and the other gods, protect us always with blessings.

##### English translation
Enable us, Indra, (to partake of) food granted by you, as well as those who, opulent (in sacrificial presentations), spontaneously offer (you oblations): may there be power in your adorer (to repeat) many laudations: and ever cherish us with blessings.

### Printed p. 14

## Sūkta 21 (Maṇḍala 7)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> असावि देवमिति दशर्चं चतुर्थं सूक्तं वसिष्ठस्यार्षं त्रैष्टुभमैंद्रं । तथा चानुक्रांतं । असावीति ॥ माध्यंदिनसवने मैत्रावरुणस्योन्नेयमानमिदं सूक्तं । असावि देवमिहोप यातेत्यनुसवनं । आ -५. ५. । इति ॥ इंद्रस्य वृत्रघ्नः पशावभि क्रत्वेति वपाया अनुवाक्या । सूत्रितं च । अभि क्रत्वेंद्र भूरध ज्मन् त्वं महा इंद्र तुभ्यं ह क्षाः । आ - ३. ८ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta असावि देवं (*asāvi devaṃ*) of ten Ṛks is the fourth sūkta; its seer is Vasiṣṭha, its metre Triṣṭubh and its deity Indra. So it is recorded in the Anukramaṇī: असावीति (*asāvīti*). In the midday pressing this sūkta is the one recited by the Maitrāvaruṇa priest as he leads up; the sūtra is असावि देवमिहोप यातेत्यनुसवनं (*asāvi devamihopa yātetyanusavanaṃ*) (Ā. 5-5). In the animal sacrifice to Indra the Vṛtra-slayer, the verse पशावभि क्रत्वेति (*paśāvabhi kratveti*) is the anuvākyā for the vapā; and it is prescribed in the sūtra अभि क्रत्वेंद्र भूरध ज्मन् त्वं महा इंद्र तुभ्यं ह क्षाः । (*abhi kratveṃdra bhūradha jman tvaṃ mahā iṃdra tubhyaṃ ha kṣāḥ |*) (Ā. 3-8).

Translation: This sūkta beginning असावि देवं (*asāvi devaṃ*) is the fourth sūkta in the second anuvāka. It contains ten Ṛks. Vasiṣṭha is the seer of this sūkta; Indra is the deity; the metre is Triṣṭubh. In the Anukramaṇī it is stated as beginning with असावि (*asāvi*). At the time of the midday pressing, the priest called Maitrāvaruṇa is to recite this sūkta as the unnīyamāna sūkta; this is set forth by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra असावि देवमिहोप यातेत्यनुसवनं (*asāvi devamihopa yātetyanusavanaṃ*) (Ā. 5-5). In the animal sacrifice made to Indra the Vṛtra-slayer, the sixth Ṛk of this sūkta, अभि क्रत्वा (*abhi kratvā*), is to be recited as the anuvākyā mantra at the vapā offering; this is set forth by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra अभि क्रत्वेंद्र भूरध ज्मंस्त्वं महा इंद्र तुभ्यं ह क्षाः (*abhi kratveṃdra bhūradha jmaṃstvaṃ mahā iṃdra tubhyaṃ ha kṣāḥ*) (Ā. 3-8).

Sūkta 21. Maṇḍala 7; Anuvāka 2; Sūkta 21. Aṣṭaka 5; Adhyāya 3; Varga 3, 4. Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 10. Seer: Vasiṣṭha. Deity: Indra. Metre: Triṣṭubh.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> असा॑वि दे॒वं गोऋ॑जीक॒मंधो॒ न्य॑स्मि॒न्निंद्रो॑ ज॒नुषे॑मुवोच ।  
> बोधा॑मसि त्वा हर्यश्व य॒ज्ञैर्बोधा॑ नः॒ स्तोम॒मंध॑सो॒ मदे॑षु ॥ १ ॥

### Printed p. 15

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> असावि । देवं । गोऋजीकं । अंधः । नि । अस्मिन् । इंद्रः । जनुषा । ईं ।  
> उवोच ।  
> बोधामसि । त्वा । हरिऽअश्व । यज्ञैः । बोध । नः । स्तोमं । अंधसः । मदेषु ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> देवं दीप्तं गोऋजीकं गोभिः संस्कृतं । गव्येन मिश्रितमित्यर्थः । अंधः सोमरूपमन्नमसावि । अभिषुतं । ईमयमिंद्रोऽस्मिन्नभिषुते सोमरूपेऽंधसि जनुषा स्वभावत एव न्युवोच । नितरां संगतो भवति । अथ प्रत्यक्षस्तुतिः । हे हर्यश्व त्वा त्वां यज्ञैः स्तोत्रैर्हविर्भिर्वा बोधामसि । बोधयामः । अंधसः सोमस्य मदेषु नोऽस्माकं स्तोमं स्तोत्रं बोध । बुध्यस्व च ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The shining (deva) Soma-food, gorjīka, prepared with cows, that is, mixed with what comes from the cow, has been pressed. This same Indra, in this pressed Soma-food, from his birth, by his very nature, is closely joined. Then follows direct address: O lord of bay horses, we awaken you with sacrifices, that is, with hymns or oblations. In the exhilarations of the Soma, understand our hymn, our praise, and awaken.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- देवं (*devaṃ*)—the shining
- गोऋजीकं (*goṛjīkaṃ*)—mixed with milk and the like
- अंधः (*aṃdhaḥ*)—the food in the form of Soma
- असावि (*asāvi*)—is pressed and ready to be offered
- ईं इंद्रः (*īṃ iṃdraḥ*)—this same Indra
- अस्मिन् (*asmin*)—with this pressed Soma juice
- जनुषा (*januṣā*)—from birth
- नि उवोच (*ni uvoca*)—is completely united
- हर्यश्व (*haryaśva*)—O Indra, possessor of the horses named Hari
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- यज्ञैः (*yajñaiḥ*)—with hymns
- बोधामसि (*bodhāmasi*)—we awaken
- अंधसः (*aṃdhasaḥ*)—as the result of drinking the Soma juice
- मदेषु (*madeṣu*)—when exhilaration arises
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- स्तोमं (*stomaṃ*)—the nature of the hymn
- बोध (*bodha*)—understand

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The shining Soma-food, mixed with milk and the like, is pressed and ready to be offered. This same Indra is completely united, from his birth, with this pressed Soma juice. O Indra, possessor of the horses named Hari, we awaken you with hymns. When exhilaration arises as the result of drinking the Soma juice, understand the force of our hymn.

##### English translation
The bright sacrificial food mixed with curds and milk has been poured out : Indra delights in it from his birth : lord of bay horses, we wake you up with sacrifices, acknowledge our praises in the exhilaration of the soma beverage.

### Printed p. 16

#### Ṛk 2

**NOTE**

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्र यं॑ति य॒ज्ञं वि॒पयं॑ति ब॒र्हिः सो॑म॒मादो॑ वि॒दथे॑ दु॒ध्र-वा॑चः ।  
> न्यू॑ भ्रियंते य॒शसो॑ गृ॒भादा दू॒रेउ॑पब्दो॒ वृष॑णो नृ॒षाचः॑

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्र । यंति । यज्ञं । विपयंति । बर्हिः । सोममादः । विदथे । दुध्रवाचः ।  
> नि । ऊं इति । भ्रियंते । यशसः । गृभात् । आ । दूरेऽउपब्दः । वृषणः । नृऽसाचः ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यज्ञं प्र यंति यष्टारो बर्हिश्च विपयंति । स्तृणंति । विपिः स्तरणकर्मा । विदथे यज्ञे सोममादो ग्रावाणश्च दुध्रवाचो दुर्धरवाचो भवंति । अपि च यशसो यशस्विनो दूरेउपब्दाः । दूर उपब्दिः शब्दो येषां ते दूरेउपब्दाः । नृषाचः । नृन्नेतॄनृत्विजः सचंते इति नृषाचः । वृषणो ग्रावाणो गृभाद्गृहात् । गृहमध्यमग्रावा । तस्मात् । आ इति चार्थे । नि भ्रियंते । अभिषववेलायां निगृह्यंते । उ इति पूरणः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sacrificers advance to the sacrifice and strew the sacred grass; vipi is a word for strewing. In the sacrifice (vidatha) the Soma-pressing stones have hard-to-restrain voices. Moreover, they are renowned, and their sound is heard from afar. The word nṛṣācaḥ means those that accompany the leaders, the priests. The showering stones are brought out from the house; **NOTE** the word आ (*ā*) is in the sense of "and". They are brought out at the time of pressing; the word उ (*u*) is an expletive.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यज्ञं (*yajñaṃ*)—the sacrificial house
- प्र यंति (*pra yaṃti*)—the sacrificers enter
- बर्हिः (*barhiḥ*)—the seat of darbha grass too
- विपयंति (*vipayaṃti*)—is spread out widely
- विदथे (*vidathe*)—in the sacrifice
- सोममादः (*somamādaḥ*)—the stones that press the Soma
- दुध्रवाचः (*dudhravācaḥ*)—have a restrained sound
- यशसः (*yaśasaḥ*)—renowned
- दूरेउपब्दः (*dūreupabdaḥ*)—sounding from afar
- नृषाचः (*nṛṣācaḥ*)—joining the priests
- वृषणः (*vṛṣaṇaḥ*)—those stones
- गृभात् (*gṛbhāt*)—from their house
- आ नि भ्रियंते उ (*ā ni bhriyaṃte u*)—are brought out only at the times of pressing

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The sacrificers enter the sacrificial house. The seat of darbha grass too is spread out widely. In the sacrifice the stones that press the Soma have a restrained sound. The stones, renowned, sounding from afar and joining the priests, are brought out from their house only at the times of pressing.

### Printed p. 17

##### English translation
They repair to the sacrifice, they strew the sacred grass : the (grinding) stones at the ceremony are of difficulty suppressed noise : famous priests, whose voices are heard far off, bring the stones from the interior of the dwelling.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> त्वमिं॑द्र॒ स्रवि॑त॒वा अ॒पस्कः॒ परि॑ष्ठिता॒ अहि॑ना शूर पू॒र्वीः ।  
> त्वद्वा॑वक्रे र॒थ्यो॒३॒॑३ न धेना॒ रेजं॑ते॒ विश्वा॑ कृ॒त्रिमा॑णि भी॒षा ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> त्वं । इंद्र । स्रवितवै । अपः । करिति कः । परिस्थिताः । अहिना । शूर । पूर्वीः ।  
> त्वत् । वावक्रे । रथ्यः । न । धेनाः । रेजंते । विश्वा । कृत्रिमाणि । भीषा ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे शूरेंद्र त्वमहिना वृत्रेण परिष्ठिता आक्रांताः पूर्वीर्बह्वीरप उदकानि स्रवितवै स्रवितुं कः । अकार्षीः । धेना नद्यश्च त्वत्त्वत्तो हेतो रथ्यो न रथिन इव वावक्रे । निर्गच्छंति ॥ वकि कौटिल्य इति धातुः ॥ विश्वा विश्वानि कृत्रिमाणि भुवनानि च भीषा त्वत्तो भीत्या रेजंते । कंपंते ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O hero Indra, you have made the many waters, held around and overcome by Ahi, that is Vṛtra, able to flow. And the rivers (dhenāḥ), because of you, come forth like charioteers; the root vak is in the sense of crookedness. All the created worlds tremble from fear of you.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- शूर (*śūra*)—O valiant one
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- अहिना (*ahinā*)—by the asura Vṛtra
- परिष्ठिताः (*pariṣṭhitāḥ*)—enveloped
- पूर्वीः (*pūrvīḥ*)—many
- आपः (*āpaḥ*)—waters
- स्रवितवै (*sravitavai*)—so that they could flow
- कः (*kaḥ*)—you have made
- धेनाः (*dhenāḥ*)—the rivers too
- त्वत् (*tvat*)—in accordance with your rule
- रथ्यो न (*rathyo na*)—like drivers of chariots
- वावक्रे (*vāvakre*)—come forth and flow
- विश्वा (*viśvā*)—all
- कृत्रिमाणि (*kṛtrimāṇi*)—the worlds too
- भीषा (*bhīṣā*)—from fear of you
- रेजंते (*rejaṃte*)—tremble

### Printed p. 18

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O valiant Indra, you have made the many waters enveloped by the asura Vṛtra able to flow. The rivers too, in accordance with your rule, come forth and flow like charioteers. All the worlds too tremble from fear of you.

##### English translation
You, hero, have enabled the many waters arrested by Ahi to flow : by you the rivers rushed forth like charioteers : all created worlds trembled through fear of you.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> भी॒मो वि॑वे॒षायु॑धेभिरेषा॒मपां॑सि॒ विश्वा॒ नर्या॑णि वि॒द्वान् ।  
> इंद्रः॒ पुरो॒ जर्हृ॑षाणो॒ वि दू॑धो॒द्वि वज्र॑हस्तो महि॒ना ज॑घान ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> भीमः । विवेष । आयुधेभिः । एषां । अपांसि । विश्वा । नर्याणि । विद्वान् ।  
> इंद्रः । पुरः । जर्हृषाणः । वि । दूधोत् । वि । वज्रहस्तः । महिना । जघान ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> इंद्रो नर्याणि नरहितानि विश्वा विश्वान्यपांसि कर्माणि विद्वान् ज्ञातवान् आयुधे-भिरायुधैर्भीमो भयंकरः सन् एषां ॥ कर्माणि षष्ठी ॥ एतानसुरान् विवेष । व्याप्तवान् । पुरश्च तेषां वि दूधोत् । आकंपयत् । अपि च जर्हृषाणो हृष्यन् महिना महिम्ना युक्तो वज्रहस्तः सन् तान् जघान ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Indra, knowing all the deeds beneficial to men, fearsome with his weapons, advanced against these asuras and shook their cities. Moreover, exulting, endowed with greatness, vajra in hand, he slew them.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—Indra
- नर्याणि (*naryāṇi*)—beneficial to men
- विश्वा अपांसि (*viśvā apāṃsi*)—all deeds
- विद्वान् (*vidvān*)—knowing
- आयुधेभिः (*āyudhebhiḥ*)—together with his weapons
- भीमः (*bhīmaḥ*)—fearsome
- एषां (*eṣāṃ*)—facing these asuras
- विवेष (*viveṣa*)—advanced
- पुरः (*puraḥ*)—their cities too
- वि दूधोत् (*vi dūdhot*)—made to tremble
- जर्हृषाणः (*jarhṛṣāṇaḥ*)—showing joy
- महिना (*mahinā*)—and endowed with greatness
- वज्रहस्तः (*vajrahastaḥ*)—holding the thunderbolt in his hand
- वि जघान (*vi jaghāna*)—slew them all

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Indra, knowing all the deeds beneficial to men, fearsome with his weapons, faced these asuras and advanced. He made their cities tremble. Showing joy, endowed with greatness, and holding the thunderbolt in his hand, he slew them all.

### Printed p. 19

##### English translation
The formidable (Indra), knowing all actions beneficial to man, intimidated those (Asuras) by his weapons : Indra, exulting, shook their cities : armed with his thunderbolt he slew them in his might.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> न या॒तव॑ इंद्र जूजुवुर्नो॒ न वंद॑ना शविष्ठ वे॒द्याभिः॑ ।  
> स श॑र्धद॒र्यो विषु॑णस्य जं॒तोर्मा शि॒श्नदे॑वा॒ अपि॑ गुर्ऋ॒तं  ॑
> नः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> न । यातवः । इंद्र । जूजुवुः । नः । न । वंदना । शविष्ठ । वेद्याभिः ।  
> सः । शर्धत् । अर्यः । विषुणस्य । जंतोः । मा । शिश्नदेवाः । अपि । गुः । ऋतं । नः ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र यातवो राक्षसा नोऽस्मान् जूजुवुः । न हिंस्युः । जूजुवुरिति हिंसाक्रियः पृथक्करणक्रियो वा । अपि च हे शविष्ठ बलवत्तमेंद्र वंदना वंदनानि रक्षांसि वेद्याभिर्वेद्याभ्यः प्रजाभ्यो नोऽस्मान्न जूजुवुः । न पृथक्कुर्वंतु । किंचार्यः स्वामी स इंद्रो विषुणस्य विषमस्य जंतोः प्राणिनः शासने शर्धत् । उत्सहेत । अथ च शिश्नदेवाः । शिश्नेन दीव्यंति क्रीडंत इति शिश्नदेवाः । अब्रह्म-चर्या इत्यर्थः । नोऽस्माकमृतं यज्ञं सत्यं वा मापि गुः । मापिगमन् । तथा च यास्कः । स उत्सहतां यो विषुणस्य जंतोर्विषमस्य मा शिश्नदेवा अब्रह्म-चर्याः । शिश्नं श्नथतेः । अपि गुर्ऋतं नः सत्यं वा यज्ञं वा । नि. ४-१९ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: O Indra, may the Rākṣasas, the यातवः (*yātavaḥ*), not harm us; जूजुवुः (*jūjuvuḥ*) is a verb of harming, or of separating. Further, O most powerful Indra, may the वंदना (*vaṃdanā*), that is the Rākṣasas, not separate us from the beings to be known (वेद्याभिः (*vedyābhiḥ*)), our offspring. Moreover, Indra, the lord (अर्यः (*aryaḥ*)), would exert himself (शर्धत् (*śardhat*)) in the rule over the crooked, disorderly creature (विषुणस्य जंतोः (*viṣuṇasya jaṃtoḥ*)). And the शिश्नदेवाः (*śiśnadevāḥ*) are those who sport with the generative organ, that is, the unchaste; may they not come upon (मापि गुः (*māpi guḥ*), "may they not go to") our rite or our truth, ऋतं (*ṛtaṃ*). He cites Yāska: "May he exert himself who is lord over the crooked creature; let not the unchaste, the शिश्नदेवा (*śiśnadevā*), come upon our ऋतं (*ṛtaṃ*), truth or rite" (Ni. 4-19). **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- यातवः (*yātavaḥ*)—the Rākṣasas
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- न जूजुवुः (*na jūjuvuḥ*)—let them not harm
- शविष्ठ (*śaviṣṭha*)—O Indra, exceedingly mighty
- वंदना (*vaṃdanā*)—that same demonic power
- वेद्याभिः (*vedyābhiḥ*)—from our sons and the rest
- न (*na*)—let it not separate us
- अर्यः (*aryaḥ*)—the lord
- सः (*saḥ*)—that same Indra
- विषुणस्य (*viṣuṇasya*)—of misshapen form
- जंतोः (*jaṃtoḥ*)—in the restraint of creatures
- शर्धत् (*śardhat*)—shows zeal (therefore)
- शिश्नदेवाः (*śiśnadevāḥ*)—the Asuras given to immoral sports
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- ऋतं (*ṛtaṃ*)—sacrifice
- मा अपि गुः (*mā api guḥ*)—will not be able to obstruct

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, let the Rākṣasas not harm us. O Indra, exceedingly mighty, let that same demonic power not separate us from our sons and the rest. That same Indra, the lord, shows zeal in the restraint of creatures of misshapen form. Therefore the Asuras, given to immoral sports, will not be able to obstruct our sacrifice.

### Printed p. 20

##### English translation
Let not the Rakshasas, Indra, do us harm ; let not the evil spirits do harm to our progeny, most powerful (Indra): let the sovereign lord, (Indra), exert himself (in the restraint) of disorderly beings, so that the unchaste may not disturb our rite.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

विषुणस्य (*viṣuṇasya*), शिश्नदेवा (*śiśnadevā*): Explaining these words, Yāska says in his Nirukta as follows:

विषुणः । स शर्धदर्यो विषुणस्य जंतोर्मा शिश्नदेवा अपि गुर्ऋतं नः । स उत्सहतां यो विषुणस्य जंतोर्विषमस्य मा शिश्नदेवा अब्रह्म-चर्याः । शिश्नं श्नथतेः । अपि गुर्ऋतं नः । सत्यं वा यज्ञं वा ॥ (*viṣuṇaḥ | sa śardhadaryo viṣuṇasya jaṃtormā śiśnadevā api gurṛtaṃ naḥ | sa utsahatāṃ yo viṣuṇasya jaṃtorviṣamasya mā śiśnadevā abrahma-caryāḥ | śiśnaṃ śnathateḥ | api gurṛtaṃ naḥ | satyaṃ vā yajñaṃ vā ‖*)

विषुणस्य (*viṣuṇasya*) means विषमस्य (*viṣamasya*): of misshapen form, having deformed limbs unlike what everyone has, or lacking understanding. Indra shows zeal in keeping such creatures under control. And शिश्नदेवाः (*śiśnadevāḥ*) means those who, without the discipline of chastity and the like, are given to evil acts such as intercourse with forbidden women and unlawful union with women, those who are outside morality. Or the meaning may also be said to be: wicked people who do not worship the gods such as Indra, but take the male organ itself for a god and are devoted to unlawful and immoral sports. The main sense of the explanation is that such people of wicked nature, who have no rule of morality, should not cause obstruction in the sacrifice we perform; that is, may Indra restrain such wicked ones and grant that our sacrifice be completed in full. The word ऋत (*ṛta*) means truth or sacrifice.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒भि क्रत्वें॑द्र भू॒रध॒ ज्मन्न ते॑ विव्यङ्महि॒मानं॒ रजां॑सि ।  
> स्वेना॒ हि वृ॒त्रं शव॑सा ज॒घंथ॒ न शत्रु॒रंतं॑ विविदद्यु॒धा ते॑ ॥

### Printed p. 21

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अभि । क्रत्वा । इंद्र । भूः । अध । ज्मन् । न । ते । विव्यक् । महिमानं । रजांसि ।  
> स्वेन । हि । वृत्रं । शवसा । जघंथ । न । शत्रुः । अंतं । विविदत् । युधा । ते ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र त्वं क्रत्वा कर्मणा ज्मन् पृथिव्यां वर्तमानान् जंतून्वाभि भूः । अभ्यभूः । अधापि च ते तव महिमानं रजांसि सर्वे लोका न विव्यक् । व्यचिर्व्याप्तिकर्मा । न व्याप्नुवन्नित्यर्थः । स्वेन ह्यात्मीयेन च शवसा बलेन वृत्रं जघंथ । त्वमवधीः । शत्रुश्च युधा युद्धेन ते तवांतं हिंसां न विविदत् । न लब्धवान् ।

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: O Indra, you by your deed (क्रत्वा (*kratvā*)) overcome (अभि भूः (*abhi bhūḥ*)) the creatures that live on the earth (ज्मन् (*jman*)). Moreover, all the worlds (रजांसि (*rajāṃsi*)) do not pervade (न विव्यक् (*na vivyak*)) your greatness; the root व्यचि (*vyaci*) has the sense of pervading, so the meaning is that they do not encompass it. By your own strength you slew Vṛtra, and no enemy found the end, that is the destruction, of you in battle.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- क्रत्वा (*kratvā*)—by your extraordinary deeds
- ज्मन् (*jman*)—all creatures on the earth
- अभि भूः (*abhi bhūḥ*)—you have brought under your sway
- अध (*adha*)—moreover
- ते (*te*)—your
- महिमानं (*mahimānaṃ*)—greatness
- रजांसि (*rajāṃsi*)—all the worlds, gathered together
- न विव्यक् (*na vivyak*)—cannot pervade (cannot obtain)
- स्वेन (*svena*)—your natural
- शवसा हि (*śavasā hi*)—by strength alone
- वृत्रं (*vṛtraṃ*)—the asura Vṛtra
- जघंथ (*jaghaṃtha*)—you destroyed
- शत्रुः (*śatruḥ*)—no enemy
- युधा (*yudhā*)—in battle
- ते (*te*)—to you
- अंतं (*aṃtaṃ*)—harm
- न विविदत् (*na vividat*)—can bring about

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, by your extraordinary deeds you have brought under your sway all creatures on the earth. Moreover, all the worlds gathered together could not surpass your greatness. By your own strength you destroyed the asura Vṛtra. No enemy can bring harm upon you in battle.

##### English translation
You, Indra, by your function, preside over the beings (of earth) : all the regions (of the world) do not surpass your magnitude : by your own strength you have slain Vritra : no enemy has effected your destruction in battle.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> दे॒वाश्चि॑त्ते असु॒र्या॑य॒ पूर्वीरनु॑ क्ष॒त्राय॑ ममिरे॒ सहां॑सि ।

### Printed p. 22

> इंद्रो मघानि दयते विषह्येंद्रं वाजस्य जोहुवंत  
> सातौ ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> देवाः । चित् । ते । असुर्याय । पूर्वे । अनु । क्षत्राय । ममिरे । सहांसि ।  
> इंद्रः । मघानि । दयते । विऽसह्य । इंद्रं । वाजस्य । जोहुवंत । सातौ ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पूर्वे देवाश्चिदसुरा अस्यसुर्याय बलाय क्षत्राय । क्षदिर्हिंसाकर्मा । बलं हिंसां चोभे कर्तुमित्यर्थः । हे इंद्र ते तव सहांसि बलान्यनु ममिरे ॥ हीने । पा-सू १-४-८६ । इत्यनु कर्मप्रवचनीयः ॥ तव बलेभ्यो हीना ममिर इत्यर्थः । तथा च निगमांतरं । अनु ते द्यौर्बृहती वीर्यं ममे । ऋ-१-५३-५ । इति । अथ परोक्षस्तुतिः । इंद्रः शत्रून्विषह्य मघानि मंहनीयानि धनानि दयते । भक्तेभ्यः प्रयच्छति । अपि चेंद्रं वाजस्यान्नस्य सातौ लाभार्थं जोहुवंत । स्तुवंति स्तोतार आह्वयंति वा ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: The earlier ones, the gods, the Asuras (देवाश्चिदसुरा (*devāścidasurā*)), measured your powers (सहांसि (*sahāṃsi*)) as inferior to the strength (असुर्याय (*asuryāya*)) and the क्षत्राय (*kṣatrāya*); the root क्षदि (*kṣadi*) has the sense of harming, so the meaning is that they were able to exercise both strength and violence. O Indra, they measured themselves as lower than your powers. He cites another Vedic text in the same sense: अनु ते द्यौर्बृहती वीर्यं ममे (*anu te dyaurbṛhatī vīryaṃ mame*) (Ṛ. 1-53-5). Then follows praise in the third person: Indra, having overcome his enemies, bestows (दयते (*dayate*)) the precious riches (मघानि (*maghāni*)) on his devotees; and the praisers call upon Indra, that is, praise or invoke him, to obtain food. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- पूर्वे (*pūrve*)—ancient
- देवाश्चित् (*devāścit*)—even the Asuras
- क्षत्राय (*kṣatrāya*)—for the violent
- असुर्याय (*asuryāya*)—than the strength
- ते (*te*)—your
- सहांसि (*sahāṃsi*)—powers are greater, thus
- अनु ममिरे (*anu mamire*)—they have acknowledged
- इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—Indra
- विषह्य (*viṣahya*)—having conquered the enemies
- मघानि (*maghāni*)—the precious riches
- दयते (*dayate*)—gives to his devotees
- इंद्रं (*iṃdraṃ*)—that same Indra
- वाजस्य (*vājasya*)—of food
- सातौ (*sātau*)—for the gaining
- जोहुवंते (*johuvaṃte*)—the praisers call with praise

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, even the ancient Asuras have acknowledged that your powers are greater than their own violent strength. Indra, having conquered his enemies, gives the precious riches to his devotees. For the gaining of food, the praisers call upon that same Indra with praise.

##### English translation
The older deities have confessed your vigour superior to their distructive strength. Indra having subdued his foes, gives the rich spoils (to his worshippers) : they invoke Indra to obtain food.

### Printed p. 23

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> की॒रिश्चि॒द्धि त्वामव॑से जु॒हावेशा॑नमिंद्र॒ सौभ॑गस्य॒ भूरेः॑ ।  
> अवो॑ बभूथ शतमूते अ॒स्मे अ॑भिक्ष॒त्तुस्त्वाव॑तो वरू॒ता ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> कीरिः । चित् । हि । त्वां । अवसे । जुहाव । ईशानं । इंद्र । सौभगस्य । भूरेः ।  
> अवः । बभूथ । शतम्ऽऊते । अस्मे इति । अभिक्षत्तुः । त्वावतः । वरूता ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र ईशानं त्वां कीरिः स्तोता । कारुः कीरिरिति स्तोतृनामसु पाठात् । वसिष्ठोऽवसे रक्षणाय जुहाव हि । स्तौति हि ह्वयति वा । चिदिति पूरणः । अपि च हे श-तमूते बहुरक्षेंद्र अस्मे अस्माकं भूरेः प्रभूतस्य सौभगस्य धनस्यावो रक्षा ब-भूथ । बभूविथ । अभिक्षत्तुरभिहिंसकस्य त्वावतस्त्वत्सदृशस्य वरूता वारयिता च भव ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: O Indra, the praiser (कीरिः (*kīriḥ*); he quotes the reading of the lists of names of praisers, "kāru, kīri"), Vasiṣṭha, has called you, the lord (ईशानं (*īśānaṃ*)), for protection (अवसे (*avase*)), that is, he praises or invokes you; चित् (*cit*) is a filler. Moreover, O Indra of a hundred aids, you have become the protector (अवः (*avaḥ*)) of our abundant good fortune, that is, of wealth. And be the warder-off (वरूता (*varūtā*)) of a harmful (अभिक्षत्तुः (*abhikṣattuḥ*)) one like yourself (त्वावतः (*tvāvataḥ*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- ईशानं (*īśānaṃ*)—the lord
- त्वां (*tvāṃ*)—you
- कीरिः (*kīriḥ*)—the praiser
- अवसे (*avase*)—for protection
- जुहाव हि (*juhāva hi*)—has called
- शतमूते (*śatamūte*)—O Indra, endowed with many protections
- अस्मे (*asme*)—our
- भूरेः (*bhūreḥ*)—exceedingly great
- सौभगस्य (*saubhagasya*)—of wealth
- अवः (*avaḥ*)—as protector
- बभूथ (*babhūtha*)—you have become
- त्वावतः (*tvāvataḥ*)—of one with strength like yours
- अभिक्षत्तुः (*abhikṣattuḥ*)—the harmful Asura
- वरूता (*varūtā*)—be the warder-off

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, the praiser has called you, the lord, for protection. O Indra, endowed with many protections, you have become the protector of our exceeding great wealth. Be the warder-off of the harmful Asura who has strength like yours.

##### English translation
The worshipper has invoked you the sovereign Indra, for protection: protector of many, you have been to us the guardian of great good fortune ; be our defender against every overpowering (assailant) like to you.

### Printed p. 24

#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> सखा॑यस्त इंद्र वि॒श्वह॑ स्याम नमोवृ॒धासो॑ महि॒ना त॑रुत्र ।  
> व॒न्वंतु॑ स्म॒ तेऽव॑सा समी॒के॒३॒॑ऽभी॑तिम॒र्यो व॒नुषां॒ शवां॑सि ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सखायः । ते । इंद्र । विश्वह । स्याम । नमःवृधासः । महिना । तरुत्र ।  
> वन्वंतु । स्म । ते । अवसा । सम्ऽईके । अभिऽइतिं । अर्यः । वनुषां । शवांसि ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र ते तव नमोवृधासो नमसा स्तुत्या हविषा वा वर्धयितारो वयं विश्वह सर्वदा सखायः स्याम । भवेम । महिना महिम्ना तरुत्रात्यंतं तार-केंद्र ते तेवावसा रक्षणेन समीके संग्रामेऽर्योऽभीतिमभिगमनं वनुषां हिंस-कानां शवांसि बलानि च वन्वंतु । स्तोतारो हिंसंतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: O Indra, we who increase you (नमोवृधासः (*namovṛdhāsaḥ*)) with homage, that is, praise or oblation, may be your friends at all times (विश्वह (*viśvaha*)). O Indra, who carry us exceedingly across (तरुत्र (*tarutra*)) by your greatness, by your protection let the enemy's (अर्यः (*aryaḥ*)) onset (अभीतिं (*abhītiṃ*)) and the strengths (शवांसि (*śavāṃsi*)) of the harmful (वनुषां (*vanuṣāṃ*)) in battle (समीके (*samīke*)) be overcome; let the praisers harm them. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- ते (*te*)—your
- नमोवृधासः (*namovṛdhāsaḥ*)—we who grow by praises
- विश्वह (*viśvaha*)—on all days
- सखायः (*sakhāyaḥ*)—as friends to you
- स्याम (*syāma*)—may we be
- महिना (*mahinā*)—by your greatness
- तरुत्र (*tarutra*)—O Indra, who protect exceedingly
- ते (*te*)—your
- अवसा (*avasā*)—by protection
- समीके (*samīke*)—in battle
- अर्यः (*aryaḥ*)—of the enemy
- अभीतिं (*abhītiṃ*)—the resistance
- वनुषां (*vanuṣāṃ*)—of the violent
- शवांसि (*śavāṃsi*)—the strengths
- वन्वंतु स्म (*vanvaṃtu sma*)—let your praisers destroy

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, may we, who grow by your praises, be your friends on all days. O Indra, who protect exceedingly by your greatness, protected by you, may your praisers in battle destroy the enemy's resistance and the strengths of the violent.

##### English translation
May we, daily increasing in reverence, be (regarded) Indra,(as) your friends : through the protection of you, surpasser in greatness, may (your worshippers) repulse the attack of the foe in battle, the strength of the malevolent.

### Printed p. 25

#### Ṛk 10

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स न॑ इंद्र॒ त्वय॑ताया इ॒षे धा॒स्त्मना॑ च॒ ये म॒घवा॑नो जु॒नंति॑ ।  
> वस्वी॒ षु ते॑ जरि॒त्रे अ॑स्तु श॒क्तिर्यू॒यं पा॑त स्व॒स्तिभिः॒ सदा॑ नः ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सः । नः । इंद्र । त्वयतायै । इषे । धाः । त्मना । च । ये । मघवानः । जुनंति ।  
> वस्वी । सु । ते । जरित्रे । अस्तु । शक्तिः । यूयं । पात । स्वस्तिभिः । सदा । नः ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र स त्वं त्वयताया इषे त्वया दत्तमन्नं भोक्तुं नोऽस्मान् धाः । धारय । ये च मघवानो हविष्मंतस्त्मना स्वयमेव जुनंति हवींषि त्वां प्रति प्रेरयंति तानपि त्वयताया इषे धाः । अपि च वस्वीष्वत्यंतं प्रशस्तासु स्तुतिषु ते तव जरित्रे स्तोत्रे मह्यं शक्तिः सामर्थ्यमस्तु । यद्वा । जरित्रे मह्यं ते तव वस्वी षु प्रशस्ता शक्तिर्दानमस्तु । स्पष्टमन्यत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: O Indra, you, for the food given by you (त्वयताया इषे (*tvayatāyā iṣe*)), sustain us (धाः (*dhāḥ*), "hold, maintain") that we may enjoy it. And those wealthy in oblations (मघवानः (*maghavānaḥ*)) who of themselves (त्मना (*tmanā*)) send their oblations towards you (जुनंति (*junaṃti*)), them also sustain for that food. Moreover, in the exceedingly excellent praises (वस्वीषु (*vasvīṣu*)) of you, let ability (शक्तिः (*śaktiḥ*)) be in me, your praiser (जरित्रे (*jaritre*)). Or else: let your excellent शक्तिः (*śaktiḥ*), that is, gift, be for me, the praiser. The rest is clear.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- त्वयतायै (*tvayatāyai*)—given by you
- इषे (*iṣe*)—to eat the food
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- धाः (*dhāḥ*)—make us worthy
- ये (*ye*)—which sacrificers
- मघवानः (*maghavānaḥ*)—being possessed of oblations
- त्मना (*tmanā*)—of their own impulse
- जुनंति (*junaṃti*)—offer oblations to you, make them also worthy of that food
- ते (*te*)—your
- जरित्रे (*jaritre*)—to me, the praiser
- शक्तिः (*śaktiḥ*)—ability
- अस्तु (*astu*)—let there be fullness (O Indra and the other gods)
- यूयं (*yūyaṃ*)—you
- सदा (*sadā*)—always
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- स्वस्तिभिः (*svastibhiḥ*)—with blessings
- पात (*pāta*)—protect

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, make us worthy to eat the food given by you. Those sacrificers who, being possessed of oblations, offer oblations to you of their own impulse, make them also worthy of that food. Let me, your praiser, have that same ability. O Indra and the other gods, always protect us with blessings.

##### English translation
Enable us, Indra, (to partake of) food granted by you, as well as those who, opulent (in sacrificial presentation), spontaneously offer the

### Printed p. 26

(oblations) : may there be ability in your adorer (to repeat) many laudations : and do you ever cherish us with blessings.

## Sūkta 22 (Maṇḍala 7)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पिबा सोममिंद्र मंदतु त्वेति नवर्चं पंचमं सूक्तं । अनुक्रम्यते च । पिब नव वैराजमृतेऽस्तामिति वसिष्ठ ऋषिः आदितोऽष्टौ विराजो नवमी त्रिष्टुप् । इंद्रो देवता ॥ दशरात्रे चतुर्थेऽहनि निष्केवल्यशस्त्रे पिबा सोममिंद्रेति षट् स्तोत्रीयानुरूपाः । सूत्रितं च । वैराजं चेत्प्लुष्यं पिबा सोममिंद्र मंदतु त्वेति षट् स्तोत्रीयानुरूपाः । आ. ७ ११ । इति महाव्रतेऽपि निष्केवल्य आद्याः षडृचः । सूत्रितं च पिबा सोममिंद्र मंदतु त्वेति षट् । आ. ५ ३-१० । इति आद्या निष्केवल्यशस्त्रयाज्या । सूत्रितं च पिबा सोममिंद्र मंदतु त्वेति याज्या । आ ५ १५ । इति ॥ चतुर्थेऽहनि माध्यंदिनसवने होत्रकशस्त्रेषु सप्त विराजस्त्रिष्टुबंताः कृत्वो कृतस्तृचः कंसनीयः तत्र न ते गिर इत्याद्याश्चतस्र ऋचः सूक्तं च न ते गिरो अपि मृष्ये तुजस्य प्र वो महे महिवृधे भरध्वं । आ. ७ ११ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: The sūkta of nine Ṛks beginning पिबा सोममिंद्र मंदतु त्वा (*pibā somamiṃdra maṃdatu tvā*) is the fifth sūkta. It is recorded in the Anukramaṇī: पिब नव वैराजमृतेऽस्तामिति (*piba nava vairājamṛte 'stāmiti*). Vasiṣṭha is the seer; the first eight Ṛks are in the Virāj metre and the ninth is Triṣṭubh; Indra is the deity. In the Daśarātra, on the fourth day, in the Niṣkevalya recitation, the six verses beginning पिबा सोममिंद्र (*pibā somamiṃdra*) are the Stotrīya-anurūpa verses; it is laid down in the sūtra वैराजं चेत्प्लुष्यं पिबा सोममिंद्र मंदतु त्वेति षट् स्तोत्रीयानुरूपाः (*vairājaṃ cetpluṣyaṃ pibā somamiṃdra maṃdatu tveti ṣaṭ stotrīyānurūpāḥ*) (Ā. 7 11). In the Mahāvrata too the first six Ṛks are used in the Niṣkevalya; it is laid down in the sūtra पिबा सोममिंद्र मंदतु त्वेति षट् (*pibā somamiṃdra maṃdatu tveti ṣaṭ*) (Ā. 5 3-10), and the first Ṛk is the yājyā of the Niṣkevalya recitation, as laid down in the sūtra पिबा सोममिंद्र मंदतु त्वेति याज्या (*pibā somamiṃdra maṃdatu tveti yājyā*) (Ā 5 15). On the fourth day, in the midday pressing, in the recitations of the Hotrakas, seven Virāj verses ending with a Triṣṭubh are made into triplets; there the four Ṛks beginning न ते गिरः (*na te giraḥ*) and the sūkta न ते गिरो (*na te giro*) are used, as laid down in the sūtra न ते गिरो अपि मृष्ये तुजस्य प्र वो महे महिवृधे भरध्वं (*na te giro api mṛṣye tujasya pra vo mahe mahivṛdhe bharadhvaṃ*) (Ā. 7 11). **NOTE**

This sūkta beginning पिबा सोममिंद्र मंदतु त्वा (*pibā somamiṃdra maṃdatu tvā*) **NOTE**: Vasiṣṭha is the seer of this sūkta; Indra is the deity; the first eight Ṛks of this sūkta are in the Virāj metre; the ninth Ṛk is in the Triṣṭubh metre. In the Stotrīya-anurūpa of the Niṣkevalya recitation of the fourth day of the sacrifice called Daśarātra, the six Ṛks beginning पिबा सोममिंद्र (*pibā somamiṃdra*) are declared to be Stotrīya-anurūpa verses. Not only that: by the Stotra-sūtra वैराजं चेत्प्लुष्यं पिबा सोममिंद्र मंदतु त्वेति षट् स्तोत्रीयानुरूपाः (*vairājaṃ cetpluṣyaṃ pibā somamiṃdra maṃdatu tveti ṣaṭ stotrīyānurūpāḥ*) (Ā. 7. 11) the first six Ṛks of this sūkta are to be used as Stotrīya-anurūpa mantras, and in the Mahāvrata also मंदतु त्वेति षट् (*maṃdatu tveti ṣaṭ*) (Ā. 5. 3-10) the first Ṛk of this sūkta is to be used as a mantra of the Niṣkevalya recitation. By the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra पिबा सोममिंद्र मंदतु त्वेति याज्या (*pibā somamiṃdra maṃdatu tveti yājyā*) (Ā. 5. 15) it is extended. In the midday pressing of the fourth day, among the recitation mantras said in the recitations of the Hotrakas, seven Ṛks of the Virāj metre are made into three kṛtas and each priest must recite one kṛta (a kṛta means three Ṛks). Then the application of the four Ṛks of this sūkta beginning न ते गिरः (*na te giraḥ*) (7.6.8) **NOTE** is explained by the Āśvalāyana Śrauta

### Printed p. 27

Sūtra, in the sūtra न ते गिरो अपि मृष्ये तुरस्य प्र वो महे महिवृधे भरध्वं (*na te giro api mṛṣye turasya pra vo mahe mahivṛdhe bharadhvaṃ*) (Ā. 7-11).

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> पिबा॒ सोम॑मिंद्र॒ मंद॑तु त्वा॒ यं ते॑ सु॒षाव॑ हर्य॒श्वाद्रिः॑ ।  
> सो॒तुर्बा॒हुभ्यां॒ सुय॑तो॒ नार्वा॑ ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> पिब । सोमं । इंद्र । मंदतु । त्वा । यं । ते । सुसाव । हरिअश्व । अद्रिः ।  
> सोतुः । बाहुभ्यां । सुयतः । न । अर्वा ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र सोमं पिब । स सोमस्त्वा त्वां मंदतु । मादयतु । हे हर्यश्व ते त्वदर्थं सोतुरभिषवकर्तुर्बाहुभ्यामर्वा न रश्मिभ्यामश्व इव सुयतः सुष्ठु परिगृहीतोऽद्रिर्ग्रावा यं सोमं सुषाव ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: O Indra, drink the Soma; may that Soma gladden you. O lord of bay horses, the stone (अद्रिः (*adriḥ*), the pressing-stone), well held (सुयतः (*suyataḥ*)) like a horse (अर्वा न (*arvā na*)) by the reins in the arms of the presser (सोतुः (*sotuḥ*)), has pressed for you the Soma that you are to drink.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- हर्यश्व (*haryaśva*)—O lord of the horse named Hari
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- ते (*te*)—for you
- सोतुः (*sotuḥ*)—of the sacrificer who presses the Soma
- बाहुभ्यां (*bāhubhyāṃ*)—by the arms
- अर्वा न (*arvā na*)—as a horse is held by the bridle
- सुयतः (*suyataḥ*)—held fast
- अद्रिः (*adriḥ*)—the pressing-stone
- यं (*yaṃ*)—which Soma
- सुषाव (*suṣāva*)—it has pressed
- सोमं (*somaṃ*)—that same Soma
- पिब (*piba*)—drink
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- मंदतु (*maṃdatu*)—may it (that Soma) gladden

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, lord of the horse named Hari, drink that same Soma which the pressing-stone (the stone for pressing), held fast like a horse held by the bridles, in the arms of the sacrificer who presses the Soma for you, has pressed. May that Soma gladden you.

### Printed p. 28

##### English translation
Drink, Indra, the Soma : may it exhilarate you, that which the stone tightly held like a horse (by the reins), by the arms of the grinder, has expressed, lord of bay horses, for you,

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यस्ते॒ मदो॒ युज्य॒श्चारु॒रस्ति॒ येन॑ वृ॒त्राणि॑ हर्यश्व॒ हंसि॑ ।  
> स त्वामिं॑द्र प्रभूवसो ममत्तु ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यः । ते । मदः । युज्यः । चारुः । अस्ति । येन । वृत्राणि । हरिअश्व । हंसि ।  
> सः । त्वां । इंद्र । प्रभुवसो इति प्रभु-वसो । ममत्तु ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे हर्यश्व ते तव यो युज्योऽनुगुणश्चारुः समीचीनो मदो मदकरः सोमोऽस्ति विद्यते येन च पीतेन सोमेन वृत्राणि हंसि हे प्रभूवसो प्रभूतधनेंद्र त्वां स सोमो ममत्तु । मादयतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: O lord of bay horses, the Soma that is yours, suited to you (युज्यः (*yujyaḥ*), "agreeable"), fair (चारुः (*cāruḥ*), "excellent") and intoxicating, and by drinking which you slay the Vṛtras, the enemies: O Indra of abundant wealth (प्रभूवसो (*prabhūvaso*)), may that Soma gladden you.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- प्रभूवसो (*prabhūvaso*)—possessed of abundant wealth
- हर्यश्व (*haryaśva*)—and lord of the horse named Hari
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- ते (*te*)—for you
- यः (*yaḥ*)—which Soma
- युज्यः (*yujyaḥ*)—is suitable
- चारुः (*cāruḥ*)—charming
- मदः (*madaḥ*)—and intoxicating
- अस्ति (*asti*)—is
- येन (*yena*)—by which drinking of the juice
- वृत्राणि (*vṛtrāṇi*)—enemies
- हंसि (*haṃsi*)—you slay
- सः (*saḥ*)—such a Soma
- त्वां (*tvāṃ*)—you
- ममत्तु (*mamattu*)—may it gladden

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, possessed of abundant wealth and lord of the horse named Hari, may that Soma gladden you which is suitable for you, and charming and intoxicating, and by drinking the juice of which you become able to slay your enemies.

##### English translation
May the exhilarating beverage which is fit for and suitable to you, by which, lord of bay horses, you slay Yritras, exhilarate you, Indra, abounding in riches.

### Printed p. 29

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> बोधा॒ सु मे॑ मघव॒न्वाच॒मेमां यां ते॒ वसि॑ष्ठो॒ अर्च॑ति॒ प्रश॑स्तिं ।  
> इ॒मा ब्रह्म॑ सध॒मादे॑ जुषस्व ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> बोध । सु । मे । मघवन् । वाचं । आ । इमां । यां । ते । वसिष्ठः । अर्चति । प्रशस्तिं ।  
> इमा । ब्रह्म । सधमादे । जुषस्व ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे मघवन् धनवन्निंद्र ते तव प्रशस्तिं स्तुतिरूपां यां वाचं वसिष्ठोऽर्चति वदति तामिमां मे वसिष्ठस्य संबंधिनीं वाचं स्वा बोध । सुष्ठ्वभिबुध्यस्व । किंचेमेमानि ब्रह्म ब्रह्माणि सधमादे यज्ञे जुषस्व । सेवस्व ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: O wealthy Indra, the speech in the form of praise of you that Vasiṣṭha recites: this speech of me, Vasiṣṭha, understand well (बोध (*bodha*)). Moreover, enjoy (जुषस्व (*juṣasva*), "partake of") these prayers (ब्रह्म (*brahma*)) at the sacrifice (सधमादे (*sadhamāde*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मघवन् (*maghavan*)—O Indra, the wealthy
- ते (*te*)—your
- प्रशस्तिं (*praśastiṃ*)—in the form of praise
- यं वाचं (*yaṃ vācaṃ*)—which word of praise
- वसिष्ठः (*vasiṣṭhaḥ*)—Vasiṣṭha
- अर्चति (*arcati*)—offers
- मे (*me*)—of me, named Vasiṣṭha
- इमां (*imāṃ*)—this word of praise
- सु आ बोध (*su ā bodha*)—understand thoroughly
- इमा ब्रह्म (*imā brahma*)—these hymns
- सधमादे (*sadhamāde*)—at the sacrifice
- जुषस्व (*juṣasva*)—partake of

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O wealthy Indra, understand, by considering it, this word of praise of me, called Vasiṣṭha, which Vasiṣṭha offers to you in the form of praise. Partake of all these hymns at the sacrifice.

##### English translation
Understand thoroughly, Maghavan, this my speech, this praise of you, which Vasishta recites ; be pleased by these prayers at the sacrifice.

### Printed p. 30

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> श्रु॒धी हवं॑ विपिपा॒नस्याद्रे॒र्बोधा॒ विप्र॒स्यार्च॑तो मनी॒षां ।  
> कृ॒ष्वा दुवां॒स्यंत॑मा॒ सचे॒मा ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> श्रुधि । हवं । विपिपानस्य । अद्रेः । बोध । विप्रस्य । अर्चतः । मनीषां ।  
> कृष्व । दुवांसि । अंतमा । सचा । इमा ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र विपिपानस्य विपीतवतो वा ममाद्रेर्ग्राव्णो हवमाह्वानं श्रुधि । शृणु । तथा च निगमांतरं । ग्रावभ्यो वाचं वदता वदद्भ्यः । ऋ. १०-९४-१ । इति । विप्रस्य प्राज्ञस्य वसिष्ठस्यार्चतः स्तुवतो मनीषां स्तुतिं बोध । बुध्यस्व च । इमेमानि क्रियमाणानि दुवांसि परिचरणान्यंतमांतिकतमानि बुद्धिस्थानि सचा सह सहायभूतः सन्ना कृष्व । कुरु च ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: O Indra, hear the call (हवं (*havaṃ*)) of the stone (अद्रेः (*adreḥ*), the pressing-stone) that has drunk (विपिपानस्य (*vipipānasya*)). He cites another text to the same effect: ग्रावभ्यो वाचं वदता वदद्भ्यः (*grāvabhyo vācaṃ vadatā vadadbhyaḥ*) (Ṛ. 10-94-1). Understand the praise (मनीषां (*manīṣāṃ*)) of the wise Vasiṣṭha who praises you, and do these services (दुवांसि (*duvāṃsi*), "attendances") that are being performed, the nearest ones (अंतमा (*aṃtamā*)), fixed in the mind, being together with us (सचा (*sacā*)) as a helper.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- विपिपानस्य (*vipipānasya*)—of this stone that drinks the Soma (O Indra)
- अद्रेः (*adreḥ*)—of the stone
- हवं (*havaṃ*)—the call
- श्रुधि (*śrudhi*)—hear
- अर्चतः (*arcataḥ*)—of him who worships you
- विप्रस्य (*viprasya*)—and is wise, of Vasiṣṭha
- मनीषां (*manīṣāṃ*)—the praise
- बोध (*bodha*)—understand (examine and accept)
- इमा दुवांसि (*imā duvāṃsi*)—these my acts of worship
- अंतमा सचा (*aṃtamā sacā*)—in the depth of your heart
- कृष्व (*kṛṣva*)—cause to enter

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, hear the call (the sound) of the stone that drinks the Soma, that is, the stone that crushes the Soma plants. Examine and accept the praise of Vasiṣṭha, who worships you and is wise. Cause these my acts of worship to enter within your heart.

##### English translation
Hear the invocation of the (grinding) stone, (of me) repeatedly drinking (the Soma), comprehend the hymn of the adoring sage, and, friendly (with us), take to your near consideration these adorations.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> न ते॒ गिरो॒ अपि॑ मृष्ये तु॒रस्य॒ न सु॑ष्टु॒तिम॑सु॒र्य॑स्य वि॒द्वान् ।

### Printed p. 31

> सदा ते नाम स्वयशो विवक्मि ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> न । ते । गिरः । अपि । मृष्ये । तुरस्य । न । सुस्तुतिं । असुर्यस्य । विद्वान् ।  
> सदा । ते । नाम । स्वयशः । विवक्मि ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र तुरस्य शत्रूणां हिंसकस्य ते तव गिरः स्तुतीरसुर्यस्य । द्वितीयार्थे षष्ठी । त्वदीयमसुर्यं बलं विद्वाञ्जानन्नहं नापि मृष्ये । मृषिर्मार्जनकर्मा । न मार्जयामि । न परित्यजामीत्यर्थः । सुष्टुतिं शोभनां स्तुतिं च नापि मृष्ये । मृषेर्मार्जनकर्मत्वमन्यत्रापि दृश्यते । तद्यथा । मा नो अग्ने सख्या पित्र्याणि प्र मर्षिष्ठाः । ऋ. १-७१-१० । इति । किंतु स्वयशोऽसाधारणयशस्ते तव नाम स्तोत्रं सदैव विवक्मि । ब्रवीमि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: O Indra, you who are the destroyer of enemies (तुरस्य (*turasya*)): knowing your strength (असुर्यस्य (*asuryasya*)), I do not wipe away (मृष्ये (*mṛṣye*)) your praises (गिरः (*giraḥ*)). The root मृषि (*mṛṣi*) has the sense of wiping off; the meaning is "I do not wipe off, I do not abandon". Nor do I wipe away the good praise (सुष्टुतिं (*suṣṭutiṃ*)). That मृषि (*mṛṣi*) means wiping off is seen elsewhere too, as in मा नो अग्ने सख्या पित्र्याणि प्र मर्षिष्ठाः (*mā no agne sakhyā pitryāṇi pra marṣiṣṭhāḥ*) (Ṛ. 1-71-10). Rather, I always proclaim (विवक्मि (*vivakmi*)) your name, that is, your hymn, whose fame (स्वयशः (*svayaśaḥ*)) is peculiar to yourself.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- तुरस्य (*turasya*)—(O Indra) of you, the destroyer of enemies
- ते (*te*)—your
- असुर्यस्य (*asuryasya*)—mighty strength
- विद्वान् (*vidvān*)—I who know
- गिरः (*giraḥ*)—your praises
- न अपि मृष्ये (*na api mṛṣye*)—I never stop
- सुष्टुतिं (*suṣṭutiṃ*)—the most excellent praise
- न (*na*)—I certainly do not stop
- सदा (*sadā*)—always
- ते (*te*)—your
- स्वयशः (*svayaśaḥ*)—praising the extraordinary fame
- नाम (*nāma*)—the hymn
- विवक्मि (*vivakmi*)—I recite

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, I who know the mighty strength of you, the destroyer of enemies, never stop reciting your praises. I certainly do not stop the most excellent praise. I always recite the hymn that praises your extraordinary fame.

##### English translation
Knowing of your strength, I refrain not from the praise nor from the glorification of you, the destroyer (of foes), but ever proclaim your especial care.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> भूरि॒ हि ते॒ सव॑ना॒ मानु॑षेषु॒ भूरि॑ मनी॒षी ह॑वते॒ त्वामित् ।  
> मारे अ॒स्मन्म॑घव॒न्ज्योक्कः॑ ॥ ६ ॥

### Printed p. 32

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> भूरि । हि । ते । सवना । मानुषेषु । भूरि । मनीषी । हवते । त्वां । इत् ।  
> मा । आरे । अस्मत् । मघवन् । ज्योक् । करिति कः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे मघवन् ते तव सवना सवनानि सोमाभिषवणानि भूरि भूरीणि मानुषेष्वस्मासु वर्तंत इति शेषः । मनीषी स्तोता त्वामित्त्वामेव भूरि हवते । नितरां ह्वयति । स्तौति । अतोऽस्मदस्मत्त आरे दूरे ज्योक् चिरकालं मा कः । आत्मानं मा कार्षीः । क्षिप्रमात्मानमस्मदासन्नं कुर्वित्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: O Maghavan, your many Soma-pressings (सवना (*savanā*)) are going on among us men (मानुषेषु (*mānuṣeṣu*)), the verb being understood. The praiser (मनीषी (*manīṣī*)) calls on you alone, very often (भूरि हवते (*bhūri havate*)); that is, he praises you. Therefore do not make yourself (मा कः (*mā kaḥ*)) far (आरे (*āre*)) from us for a long time (ज्योक् (*jyok*)); the meaning is: quickly bring yourself near to us.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मघवन् (*maghavan*)—O wealthy Indra
- मानुषेषु (*mānuṣeṣu*)—among men
- ते (*te*)—performed for you
- सवना (*savanā*)—the sacrificial rites
- भूरि (*bhūri*)—are exceedingly many
- मनीषी (*manīṣī*)—the praiser also
- त्वामित् (*tvāmit*)—you alone
- भूरि हि (*bhūri hi*)—abundantly
- हवते (*havate*)—praises (therefore)
- अस्मात् (*asmāt*)—than us
- आरे (*āre*)—being far away
- ज्योक् (*jyok*)—for a long time
- मा कः (*mā kaḥ*)—do not make us wait

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O wealthy Indra, the sacrificial rites performed for you are exceedingly many among men. The praiser also praises you alone abundantly. Therefore do not, being far away from us, make us wait for a long time.

##### English translation
Many are the sacrifices offered, Maghavan, to you amongst mankind; constantly does the worshipper indeed invoke you , therefore be not far, nor be a long time from us.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तुभ्येदि॒मा सव॑ना शूर॒ विश्वा॒ तुभ्यं॒ ब्रह्मा॑णि॒ वर्ध॑ना कृणोमि ।  
> त्वं नृभि॒र्हव्यो॑ वि॒श्वधा॑सि ॥ ७ ॥

### Printed p. 33

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तुभ्य । इत् । इमा । सवना । शूर । विश्वा । तुभ्यं । ब्रह्माणि । वर्धना । कृणोमि ।  
> त्वं । नृभिः । हव्यः । विश्वधा । असि ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे शूर तुभ्येत्तुभ्यमेवेमेमानि विश्वा विश्वानि सवना सोमाभिषवणानि मया क्रियंते इति शेषः । तुभ्यं त्वदर्थमेव वर्धना वर्धनानि ब्रह्माणि स्तोत्राणि कृणोमि । करोमि । त्वमेव नृभिर्यज्ञानां नेतृभिर्विश्वधा सर्वप्रकारैर्हव्यो ह्वातव्यः स्तुत्यो वासि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: O hero, for you alone (तुभ्येत् (*tubhyet*)) all these Soma-pressings (सवना (*savanā*)) are performed by me, the verb being understood. For you alone I make (कृणोमि (*kṛṇomi*)) hymns (ब्रह्माणि (*brahmāṇi*)), that is, praises, that cause growth (वर्धना (*vardhanā*)). You alone, by the leaders of the sacrifices (नृभिः (*nṛbhiḥ*)), in every manner (विश्वधा (*viśvadhā*)), are to be invoked or praised (हव्यः (*havyaḥ*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- शूर (*śūra*)—O valiant Indra
- तुभ्येत् (*tubhyet*)—directed to you alone
- विश्वा (*viśvā*)—all
- इमा सवना (*imā savanā*)—these sacrificial rites (are performed)
- तुभ्यं (*tubhyaṃ*)—for you alone
- वर्धना (*vardhanā*)—ever-growing
- ब्रह्माणि (*brahmāṇi*)—hymns
- कृणोमि (*kṛṇomi*)—I compose
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you alone
- नृभिः (*nṛbhiḥ*)—by the performers of the sacrifice
- विश्वथा (*viśvathā*)—in many ways
- हव्यः (*havyaḥ*)—as one to be worshipped
- असि (*asi*)—you are

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O valiant Indra, all these sacrificial rites, directed to you alone, are performed. I compose these ever-growing hymns to offer to you. You alone are worshipped by the performers of the sacrifice in many ways.

##### English translation
To you, hero, I indeed offer these sacrifices, to you I address these elevating praises : you are to be in all ways invoked by the leaders (of rites).

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> नू चि॒न्नु ते॒ मन्य॑मानस्य द॒स्मोद॑श्नुवंति महि॒मान॑मुग्र ।  
> न वी॒र्य॑मिंद्र ते॒ न राधः॑ ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> नु । चित् । नु । ते । मन्यमानस्य । दस्म । उत् । अश्नुवंति । महिमानं । उग्र ।  
> न । वीर्यं । इंद्र । ते । न । राधः ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

### Printed p. 34

> हे दस्म दर्शनीय मन्यमानस्य स्तूयमानस्य ते तव महिमानं । नू चिदिति प्रतिषेधार्थः । नु क्षिप्रं नू चिदुदश्नुवंति । केचन न प्राप्नुवंति । हे उग्रोद्गूर्ण ते तव राधो धनं नोदश्नुवंति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: O Dasma, of fair aspect, of your greatness (महिमानं (*mahimānaṃ*)), you who are praised (मन्यमानस्य (*manyamānasya*)): नू चित् (*nū cit*) has the sense of denial; नु (*nu*) means quickly; none (केचन (*kecana*)) attains it (उदश्नुवंति (*udaśnuvaṃti*)). O fierce one, uplifted one (उग्र (*ugra*)), your wealth (राधः (*rādhaḥ*)) too they do not attain.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- दस्म (*dasma*)—O Indra of attractive form
- मन्यमानस्य (*manyamānasya*)—worthy of praise
- ते (*te*)—your
- महिमानं (*mahimānaṃ*)—greatness
- नू चित् (*nū cit*)—certainly
- नु उदश्नुवंति (*nu udaśnuvaṃti*)—none can obtain
- उग्र (*ugra*)—possessed of exceeding strength
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- ते (*te*)—your
- राधः (*rādhaḥ*)—wealth
- न (*na*)—none can obtain
- वीर्यं (*vīryaṃ*)—your valour too
- न (*na*)—none can attain

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra of attractive form, certainly no one can obtain your greatness, you who are worthy of praise. O Indra, possessed of exceeding strength, no one can obtain even your wealth. It is not possible for anyone to attain your valour.

##### English translation
Indra, of goodly aspect, none attain the greatness of you who are to be honoured, nor, fierce Indra, your heroism, nor your wealth.

#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ये च॒ पूर्व॒ ऋष॑यो॒ ये च॒ नूत्ना॒ इंद्र॒ ब्रह्मा॑णि ज॒नयं॑त॒ विप्राः॑ ।  
> अ॒स्मे ते॑ संतु स॒ख्या शि॒वानि॑ यू॒यं पा॑त स्व॒स्तिभिः॒ सदा॑ नः ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ये । च । पूर्वे । ऋषयः । ये । च । नूत्नाः । इंद्र । ब्रह्माणि । जनयंत । विप्राः ।  
> अस्मे इति । ते । संतु । सख्या । शिवानि । यूयं । पात । स्वस्तिभिः । सदा । नः ॥ ९ ॥

### Printed p. 35

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ये च पूर्वे प्राक्तना ऋषयो ये च नूत्ना नूतना विप्रा मेधाविन ऋषयो ब्रह्माणि स्तोत्राणि जनयंत अजनयंत तेष्विवास्मे अस्मास्वपि हे इंद्र ते तव सख्या सख्यानि शिवानि भद्राणि संतु । स्पष्टमन्यत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: As the ancient (पूर्वे (*pūrve*)) sages and the new (नूत्ना (*nūtnā*)) sages, the wise (विप्राः (*viprāḥ*)), who generated hymns (ब्रह्माणि (*brahmāṇi*)), that is, praises, were shown your friendship, so, O Indra, let your friendships (सख्या (*sakhyā*)) be auspicious (शिवानि (*śivāni*)), that is, beneficial, for us also. The rest is clear.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- ये च (*ye ca*)—those
- पूर्वे (*pūrve*)—ancient
- ऋषयः (*ṛṣayaḥ*)—sages
- ये च (*ye ca*)—and those
- नूत्नाः (*nūtnāḥ*)—of the present time
