- विप्राः (*viprāḥ*)—sages and wise men
- ब्रह्माणि (*brahmāṇi*)—hymns
- जनयंत (*janayaṃta*)—who composed and praised you with them (as it was in their case)
- अस्मे (*asme*)—in our case also
- ते (*te*)—your
- सख्या (*sakhyā*)—friendship
- शिवानि (*śivāni*)—as bringing blessing
- संतु (*saṃtu*)—let it be (O Indra and the other gods)
- यूयं (*yūyaṃ*)—you
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- सदा (*sadā*)—always
- स्वस्तिभिः (*svastibhiḥ*)—with blessings
- पात (*pāta*)—protect

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, as your friendship was made manifest in the case of the ancient sages, and of the sages and wise men of the present time, when they composed and recited hymns praising you, so in our case also may your friendship be a bringer of blessing. O Indra and the other gods, always protect us with blessings.

##### English translation
May your auspicious regards. Indra, be directed towards us, as they have been to those pious sages, ancient or recent, who have originated (your) praises, and do you ever cherish us with blessings.

## Sūkta 23 (Maṇḍala 7)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> उदु ब्रह्माणीति षडृचं षष्ठं सूक्तं वसिष्ठस्यार्षं त्रैष्टुभमैंद्रं । अनुक्रम्यते च । उदु षडिति ॥ अग्निष्टोमे माध्यंदिने सवने ब्राह्मणाच्छंसिशस्त्र एतत्सूक्तं । सूत्रितं च । उदु ब्रह्माण्यर्जेषी वज्री वृषभस्तुराषाळिति याज्या । अ-५-१३ । इति ॥ चातुर्विंशकेऽहनि माध्यंदिनसवने ब्राह्मणाच्छंसिशस्त्र एतदहरहःशस्यसंज्ञकं सूक्तं । सूत्रितं च । उदु ब्रह्माण्यभि तष्ट्वेवेतीतरावहरहः शस्ये । आ-७-४ । इति ॥ आहर्गणेषु द्वितीयादिष्वहःस्वेतदेव सूक्तं ॥ महाव्रतेऽपि निष्केवल्य एतत्सूक्तं । सूत्रितं च । उदु ब्रह्माण्यैरत श्रवस्या ते मह इंद्रोत्युग्रेति पंच सूक्तानि । ऐ. आ. ५-२-२ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: The sūkta of six Ṛks beginning उदु ब्रह्माणि (*udu brahmāṇi*) is the sixth sūkta; its seer is Vasiṣṭha, its metre Triṣṭubh, its deity Indra. The Anukramaṇī records it with उदु षडिति (*udu ṣaḍiti*). In the Agniṣṭoma, at the midday pressing, this sūkta belongs to the recitation of the Brāhmaṇācchaṃsin; it is laid down in the sūtra उदु ब्रह्माण्यर्जेषी वज्री वृषभस्तुराषाळिति याज्या (*udu brahmāṇyarjeṣī vajrī vṛṣabhasturāṣāḷiti yājyā*) (A-5-13). On the Chāturviṃśa day, at the midday pressing, in the recitation of the Brāhmaṇācchaṃsin, this is the sūkta called Ahar-ahaḥ-śasya; it is laid down in the sūtra उदु ब्रह्माण्यभि तष्ट्वेवेतीतरावहरहः शस्ये (*udu brahmāṇyabhi taṣṭvevetītarāvaharahaḥ śasye*) (Ā-7-4). In the Ahargaṇas, on the second and the following days, this same sūkta is used. In the Mahāvrata too this sūkta belongs to the Niṣkevalya recitation; it is laid down in the sūtra उदु ब्रह्माण्यैरत श्रवस्या ते मह इंद्रोत्युग्रेति पंच सूक्तानि (*udu brahmāṇyairata śravasyā te maha iṃdrotyugreti paṃca sūktāni*) (Ai. Ā. 5-2-2). **NOTE**

### Printed p. 36

The sūkta उदु ब्रह्माणि (*udu brahmāṇi*) is the sixth sūkta in the second anuvāka. It has six Ṛks. Vasiṣṭha is the seer of this sūkta; Indra is the deity; the metre is Triṣṭubh. In the Anukramaṇī it is stated as उदु षट् (*udu ṣaṭ*). It is set forth by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra उदु ब्रह्माण्यर्जेषी वज्री वृषभस्तुराषाळिति याज्या (*udu brahmāṇyarjeṣī vajrī vṛṣabhasturāṣāḷiti yājyā*) (Ā. 5-13) that this sūkta is applied for the recitation mantras to be recited, at the time of the midday pressing in the Agniṣṭoma sacrifice, by the priest called Brāhmaṇācchaṃsin. It is set forth by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra उदु ब्रह्माण्यभि तष्ट्वेवेतीतरावहरहःशस्ये (*udu brahmāṇyabhi taṣṭvevetītarāvaharahaḥśasye*) (Ā. 7-4) that on the day of the sacrifice called Chāturviṃśika, at the time of the midday pressing, when the priest called Brāhmaṇācchaṃsin recites the recitation mantras, this sūkta is to be recited as the sūkta called Śasya, to be recited every day; and in the Ahargaṇas this sūkta is to be recited from the second day onwards. It is indicated by the sentence of the Aitareya Āraṇyaka उदु ब्रह्माण्यैरत श्रवस्या ते मह इंद्रोत्युग्रेति पंच सूक्तानि (*udu brahmāṇyairata śravasyā te maha iṃdrotyugreti paṃca sūktāni*) that in the Mahāvrata sacrifice also this sūkta is applied for the Niṣkevalya recitation mantras.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उदु॒ ब्रह्मा॑ण्यैरत श्रव॒स्येंद्रं॑ सम॒र्ये म॑हया वसिष्ठ ।  
> आ यो विश्वा॑नि॒ शव॑सा त॒तानो॑पश्रो॒ता म॒ ईव॑तो॒ वचां॑सि ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उत् । ऊं इति । ब्रह्माणि । ऐरत । श्रवस्या । इंद्रं । समर्ये । महय । वसिष्ठ ।  
> आ । यः । विश्वानि । शवसा । ततान । उपश्रोता । मे । ईवतः । वचांसि ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> श्रवस्यान्नेच्छया ब्रह्माणि स्तोत्राणि हवींषि चेंद्रार्थमुदैरत सर्व ऋषय इति शेषः । उ इति पूरणः । हे वसिष्ठ त्वमपि समर्ये यज्ञ इंद्रं महय ।

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: In desire of food or fame (श्रवस्या (*śravasyā*)) all the sages raise up hymns (ब्रह्माणि (*brahmāṇi*)), that is, praises and oblations, for Indra, the sentence being completed thus; उ (*u*) is a filler. O Vasiṣṭha, you also honour (महय (*mahaya*)) Indra at the sacrifice (समर्ये (*samarye*)).

### Printed p. 37

> स्तोत्रेण हविषा च पूजय । अपि च य इंद्रो विश्वानि भुवनानि शवसा बलेना
> ततान स ईवत उपगमनवतो मे मम वचांसि स्तुतिरूपाणि वाक्यान्युपश्रोता
> भवतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa continues: Indra is to be worshipped with praise and with oblation. Further, Indra who by his might (शवसा (*śavasā*)), that is, by his strength, has spread out all the worlds, may he become the hearer of my words, the words in the form of praise, spoken by me who approach him.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- श्रवस्या (*śravasyā*)—with desire for food
- ब्रह्माणि (*brahmāṇi*)—hymns
- उद्वैरत (*udvairata*)—the sages recited, addressing Indra
- वसिष्ठ (*vasiṣṭha*)—O Vasiṣṭha
- समर्ये (*samarye*)—at the sacrifice
- इंद्रं (*iṃdraṃ*)—Indra
- महय (*mahaya*)—worship, you also, with praises and the like
- यः (*yaḥ*)—which Indra
- विश्वानि (*viśvāni*)—all the worlds
- शवसा (*śavasā*)—by his strength
- आ ततान (*ā tatāna*)—caused to spread out (that same Indra)
- ईवतः (*īvataḥ*)—of me, who approach and worship him
- मे (*me*)—my
- वचांसि (*vacāṃsi*)—words in the form of praise
- उपश्रोता (*upaśrotā*)—let him be the hearer

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The earlier sages recited hymns addressing Indra, in desire of food. O Vasiṣṭha, at this sacrifice you also worship Indra with praises and the like. May that Indra who by his strength caused all the worlds to spread out be the hearer of my words, in the form of praise, spoken by me who approach and worship him.

##### English translation
(The sages) have offered prayers to (Indra) for food; worship Indra; Vasishtha, at the sacrifice : may that Iadra who has spread out all (the regions) by his might, be the hearer of my words when approaching him.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अया॑मि॒ घोष॑ इंद्र दे॒वजा॑मिरिर॒ज्यंत॒ यच्छु॒रुधो॒ विवा॑चि ।  
> न॒हि स्वमायु॑श्चिकि॒ते जने॑षु॒ तानीदंहां॒स्यति॑ पर्ष्य॒स्मान् ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अयामि । घोषः । इंद्र । देवजामिः । इरज्यंत । यत् । शुरुधः । विवाचि ।  
> नहि । स्वं । आयुः । चिकिते । जनेषु । तानि । इत् । अंहांसि । अति । पर्षि । अस्मान् ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यद्यदा शुरुधः । शुचं संरुंधंतीति शुरुध ओषध्यः । इरज्यंते वर्धंते
> तदा हे इंद्र त्वदर्थं विवाचि स्तोतृभि देवजामिर्देवानां बंधुर्घोषः । स्तुति-

### Printed p. 38

> रूपः शब्दो घोषः । अयामि । अकारि । अपि च जनेषु मध्ये केनापि स्वमायुः
> स्वजीवितं नहि चिकिते । न ज्ञायते । यैरायुः क्षीयते तानीत्तानि सर्वाण्येवांहांसि पापान्यस्मानति पर्षि । अतिपारय ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: When (यत् (*yat*)) the Śurudhaḥ grow, then, O Indra, a sound of praise, a kinsman of the gods, is raised for you by the worshippers. The Śurudhaḥ are the plants, so called because they check (saṃrundhanti) sorrow (śuc). The word अयामि (*ayāmi*) means "has been made". And among men no one knows his own life-span. Carry us across all those sins by which life is brought to an end.
**NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- यत् (*yat*)—when
- शुरुधः (*śurudhaḥ*)—the plants
- इरज्यंते (*irajyaṃte*)—grow (then)
- विवाचि (*vivāci*)—in the worshipper
- देवजामिः (*devajāmiḥ*)—dear to the gods
- घोषः (*ghoṣaḥ*)—the sound in the form of praise
- अयामि (*ayāmi*)—is made manifest
- जनेषु (*janeṣu*)—among men, by anyone
- स्वं (*svaṃ*)—his own (measure of life)
- नहि चिकिते (*nahi cikite*)—is surely not known
- आयुः (*āyuḥ*)—the life of man is brought to an end (by which sins)
- तानीत् अंहांसि (*tānīt aṃhāṃsi*)—from all such sins
- अस्मान् (*asmān*)—us
- अति पर्षि (*ati parṣi*)—carry across

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, when the plants grow, then a sound in the form of praise, dear to the gods, is made manifest in the worshipper. By no one among men is the measure of his own life known with certainty. Carry us across all those sins by which the life of man is brought to an end.

##### English translation
When, Indra the plants grow up, the sound (of praise) acceptable to the gods, (uttered) by the worshipper, has been raised : by no one among men, is his own life understood ; convey us beyound all those sins (by which life is shortened).

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यु॒जे रथं॑ ग॒वेष॑णं॒ हरि॑भ्या॒मुप॒ ब्रह्मा॑णि जुजुषा॒णम॑स्थुः ।  
> वि बा॑धिष्ट॒ स्य रोद॑सी महि॒त्वेंद्रो॑ वृ॒त्राण्य॑प्र॒ती ज॑घ॒न्वान् ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> युजे । रथं । गोऽएषणं । हरिऽभ्यां । उप । ब्रह्माणि । जुजुषाणं । अस्थुः ।  
> वि । बाधिष्ट । स्यः । रोदसी इति । महिऽत्वा । इंद्रः । वृत्राणि । अप्रति । जघन्वान् ॥

### Printed p. 39

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> गवेषणं गवां प्रापकमिंद्रस्य रथं हरिभ्यामिंद्रवाहाभ्यां युजे । स्तोत्रैरहं युनज्मि । ब्रह्माणि स्तोत्राणि जुजुषाणं परिचार्यैः सेव्यमानमिंद्रमुपास्थुः । उपातिष्ठंते । स्य सोऽयमिंद्रो महित्वा महत्त्वेन रोदसी द्यावापृथिव्यौ वि बाधिष्ट । व्यबाधिष्ट च । अपि चेंद्रो वृत्राणि शत्रूनप्रति द्वंद्वानि जघन्वान् हतवान् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: I yoke with praises the chariot of Indra, the cow-winning chariot (गवेषणं (*gaveṣaṇaṃ*)), to his two horses (हरिभ्यां (*haribhyāṃ*)), the bearers of Indra. The hymns approach Indra, who is pleased (जुजुषाणं (*jujuṣāṇaṃ*)) and attended by his servants. This same Indra by his greatness overcame heaven and earth, the two worlds (रोदसी (*rodasī*)). And Indra slew the Vṛtras, that is the enemies, who had no match or equal.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- गवेषणं (*gaveṣaṇaṃ*)—of Indra, who brings the winning of cows
- रथं (*rathaṃ*)—the chariot
- हरिभ्यां (*haribhyāṃ*)—with the horses
- युजे (*yuje*)—I cause to be joined (by my praises)
- ब्रह्माणि (*brahmāṇi*)—the praises
- जुजुषाणं (*jujuṣāṇaṃ*)—Indra, who is satisfied by them
- उपास्थुः (*upāsthuḥ*)—have approached
- स्यः (*syaḥ*)—that famous Indra
- महित्वा (*mahitvā*)—by his greatness
- रोदसी (*rodasī*)—heaven and earth, both
- वि बाधिष्ट (*vi bādhiṣṭa*)—has surpassed
- इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—that same Indra
- वृत्राणि (*vṛtrāṇi*)—the enemies
- अप्रति (*aprati*)—without resistance
- जघन्वान् (*jaghanvān*)—has slain

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

I praise and join the chariot of Indra, who brings the winning of cows, to his horses. For the satisfaction of Indra the praises approach him. That famous Indra has by his greatness surpassed both heaven and earth. That same Indra has destroyed the enemies by his irresistible strength.

##### English translation
I harness (by praises) the cows-bestowing chariot (of Indra) with his horses : (my) prayers have reached him who is pleased (by devotion): he has surpassed in magnitude heaven and earth, slaying the unresisting enemies.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आप॑श्चित्पिप्युः स्त॒र्यो॒३॒॑३ न गावो॒ नक्ष॑न्नृ॒तं ज॑रि॒तार॑स्त इंद्र ।  
> या॒हि वा॒युर्न नि॒युतो॑ नो॒ अच्छा॒ त्वं हि धी॒भिर्दय॑से॒ वि वाजा॑न् ॥ ४ ॥

### Printed p. 40

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आपः । चित् । पिप्युः । स्तर्यः । न । गावः । नक्षन् । ऋतं । जरितारः । ते । इंद्र ।  
> याहि । वायुः । न । नियुतः । नः । अच्छ । श्वं । हि । धीभिः । दयसे । वि । वाजान् ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र  सादादापश्चिदापः स्तर्यो न गावः स्तर्यो वशा गाव इव
> पिप्युः । वर्धंतां । असूता गावो मांसला भवंति हि । ते तव जरितारः स्तोतारश्चर्कुमुदकं नक्षन् । व्याप्नुवन् । असि च त्वं नोऽस्मान्नियुतो वायुर्न वायुरिवाच्छ याहि । अभियाहि । श्वं हि धीभिः प्रज्ञाभिः कर्मभिर्वा वाजानन्नानि वि
> दयसे । स्तोतृभ्यः प्रयच्छसि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: O Indra, may the waters also swell like the स्तर्यः (*staryaḥ*) cows; स्तर्यः (*staryaḥ*) are barren cows, and cows that have not calved become full-bodied. May your praisers reach the water (ऋतं (*ṛtaṃ*) is glossed as water). And you, come to us like the wind with its Niyut horses; come towards us. For by your insight, or by our rites, you give (दयसे (*dayase*)) food (वाजान् (*vājān*)) to the worshippers.
**NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- स्तर्यः (*staryaḥ*)—in the state of heifers
- गावः न (*gāvaḥ na*)—as cows become full-bodied
- आपः चित् (*āpaḥ cit*)—let the waters also
- पिप्युः (*pipyuḥ*)—flow fully
- ते (*te*)—your
- जरितारः (*jaritāraḥ*)—praisers
- ऋतं (*ṛtaṃ*)—water
- नक्षन् (*nakṣan*)—let them obtain
- नियुतः (*niyutaḥ*)—joined with the Niyuts
- वायुः न (*vāyuḥ na*)—as the wind comes swiftly
- नः अच्छ (*naḥ accha*)—facing us
- याहि (*yāhi*)—be gracious
- श्वं हि (*śvaṃ hi*)—you indeed
- धीभिः (*dhībhiḥ*)—satisfied by the rites
- वाजान् (*vājān*)—foods
- वि दयसे (*vi dayase*)—you give to the praisers

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, as cows in the state of heifers (that have not yet calved) become full-bodied, so let the waters also flow fully. Let your praisers obtain water. As the wind joined with the horses called Niyut comes swiftly, so be gracious, facing us. You, satisfied by our rites, are the giver of food to the praisers.

##### English translation
May the waters increase like young : may your worshippers, Indra, possess water (in abundance); come like the wind with the Niyut steeds, for you, (propitiated) by holy rites, verily bestow upon us food.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ते त्वा॒ मदा॑ इंद्र मादयंतु शु॒ष्मिणं॑ तुवि॒राध॑सं जरि॒त्रे ।  
> एको॑ देव॒त्रा दय॑से॒ हि मर्ता॑न॒स्मि ञ्छू॑र॒ सव॑ने मादयस्व ॥

### Printed p. 41

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ते । त्वा । मदाः । इंद्र । मादयंतु । शुष्मिणं । तुविराधसं । जरित्रे ।  
> एकः । देवत्रा । दयसे । हि । मर्तान् । अस्मिन् । शूर । सवने । मादयस्व ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र त्वा त्वां ते एते मदा मदकराः सोमाः मादयंतु । अपि च
> जरित्रे स्तोत्रे शुष्मिणं बलवंतं तुविराधसं बहुधनं पुत्रं प्रयच्छतीति शेषः ।
> हे शूर त्वं देवत्रा देवेष्वेक एव मर्तान्मनुष्यान् दयसे हि । दयतिरनुकंपार्थः ॥ अस्मिन्सवने यज्ञे मादयस्व ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: O Indra, may these exhilarating Soma-juices gladden you. And to the praiser they bring (the verb is to be supplied) a strong son (शुष्मिणं (*śuṣmiṇaṃ*)) of abundant wealth (तुविराधसं (*tuvirādhasaṃ*)). O hero, you alone among the gods show compassion (दयसे (*dayase*)) to mortals; the root दय (*daya*) has the sense of compassion. Be exhilarated at this sacrifice.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- मदाः (*madāḥ*)—inebriating
- ते (*te*)—these Soma-juices
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- मादयंतु (*mādayaṃtu*)—let them satisfy
- जरित्रे (*jaritre*)—to the praiser
- शुष्मिणं (*śuṣmiṇaṃ*)—a mighty
- तुविराधसं (*tuvirādhasaṃ*)—and exceedingly wealthy son, give
- शूर (*śūra*)—O valiant Indra
- देवत्रा (*devatrā*)—among all the gods
- एकः (*ekaḥ*)—you alone
- मर्तान् (*martān*)—towards men
- दयसे हि (*dayase hi*)—you show compassion
- अस्मिन् सवने (*asmin savane*)—at this sacrifice
- मादयस्व (*mādayasva*)—be satisfied

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, let these inebriating Soma-juices satisfy you. Give to the praiser a son who is mighty and exceedingly wealthy. O valiant Indra, among all the gods you alone show compassion towards men. At this sacrifice drink the Soma and be satisfied.

##### English translation
May these inebriating draughts exhilarate you, Indra : bestow upon the praiser (a son vigorous and wealthy) : for you alone amongst the gods are compassionate to mortals : be exhilarated here at this sacrifice.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ए॒वेदिंद्रं॒ वृष॑णं॒ वज्र॑बाहुं॒ वसि॑ष्ठासो अ॒भ्य॑र्चंत्य॒र्कैः ।  
> स नः॑ स्तु॒तो वी॒रव॑द्धातु॒ गोम॑द्यू॒यं पा॑त स्व॒स्तिभिः॒ सदा॑ नः ॥  
> ६

### Printed p. 42

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> एव । इत् । इंद्रं । वृषणं । वज्रऽबाहुं । वसिष्ठासः । अभि । अर्चंति । अर्कैः ।  
> सः । नः । स्तुतः । वीरऽवत् । पातु । गोऽमत् । यूयं । पात । स्वस्तिऽभिः । सदा । नः ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वसिष्ठासो वसिष्ठा वज्रबाहुं वज्रकल्पबाहुं वृषणं कामानां वर्षितारमिंद्रमेवेदुक्तेन प्रकारेणैवार्क्यैरर्चनीयैः स्तोत्रैरभ्यर्चंति । अभिपूजयंति । स्तुतः स इंद्रो नोऽस्मभ्यं वीरवत्पुत्रादियुक्तं गोमद्गोयुक्तं च धनं धातु । ददातु । स्पष्टमन्यत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: The Vasiṣṭhas (वसिष्ठासो (*vasiṣṭhāso*)) glorify Indra, the showerer of desires, whose arms are like the thunderbolt, in the manner just stated, with hymns fit for worship (अर्क्यैः (*arkyaiḥ*)); अभ्यर्चंति (*abhyarcaṃti*) means they worship. May that Indra, praised, grant us wealth that includes heroic sons and the like and that is rich in cattle (गोमत् (*gomat*)). The rest is clear.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वसिष्ठासः (*vasiṣṭhāsaḥ*)—the sages born in the family of Vasiṣṭha
- वज्रबाहुं (*vajrabāhuṃ*)—who holds the thunderbolt in his hand
- वृषणं (*vṛṣaṇaṃ*)—the showerer of desires
- इंद्रं (*iṃdraṃ*)—Indra
- एवेत् (*evet*)—in the manner stated above
- अर्क्यैः (*arkyaiḥ*)—with worshipful praises
- अभ्यर्चंति (*abhyarcaṃti*)—worship
- स्तुतः (*stutaḥ*)—praised
- सः (*saḥ*)—that same Indra
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- वीरवत् (*vīravat*)—accompanied by mighty sons
- गोमत् (*gomat*)—and rich in cattle
- धातु (*dhātu*)—let him give the wealth (O Indra and the other gods)
- यूयं (*yūyaṃ*)—you
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- सदा (*sadā*)—always
- स्वस्तिभिः (*svastibhiḥ*)—with blessings
- पात (*pāta*)—protect

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The sages born in the family of Vasiṣṭha worship, in the manner stated above, with worshipful praises Indra, who holds the thunderbolt in his hand and showers desires. May that same Indra, thus praised, give us wealth accompanied by mighty sons and rich in cattle. O Indra and the other gods, protect us always with blessings.

##### English translation
In this manner the Vasishthas glorify with hymns Indra, the showerer, the bearer of the thunderbolt : may he so glorified grant us wealth, comprising male posterity and cattle : and do you ever cherish us with blessings.

### Printed p. 43

## Sūkta 24 (Maṇḍala 7)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> योनिष्ट इंद्र सदने अकारीति षडृचं सप्तमं सूक्तं वसिष्ठस्यार्षं त्रैष्टुभमैंद्रं । अनुक्रम्यते च । योनिरिति ॥ महाव्रते निष्केवल्य एतत्सूक्तं । सूत्रितं
> च । योनिष्ट इंद्र सदने अकारीत्येतस्य चतस्रः शस्त्वोत्तमामुपसंतन्वोत्तमया परिदधाति । ऐ. आ. ५-३-१ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: The sūkta of six Ṛks beginning योनिष्ट इंद्र सदने अकारि (*yoniṣṭa iṃdra sadane akāri*) is the seventh sūkta; its seer is Vasiṣṭha, its metre Triṣṭubh, its deity Indra. The Anukramaṇī records it with योनिरिति (*yoniriti*). In the Mahāvrata this sūkta is used for the niṣkevalya recitation. It is laid down by the sūtra योनिष्ट इंद्र सदने अकारीत्येतस्य चतस्रः शस्त्वोत्तमामुपसंतन्वोत्तमया परिदधाति (*yoniṣṭa iṃdra sadane akārītyetasya catasraḥ śastvottamāmupasaṃtanvottamayā paridadhāti*) (Ai. Ā. 5-3-1).

The sūkta योनिष्ट इंद्र सदने अकारि (*yoniṣṭa iṃdra sadane akāri*) is the seventh sūkta in the second anuvāka. It has six Ṛks. Vasiṣṭha is the seer of this sūkta; Indra is the deity; the metre is Triṣṭubh. In the Anukramaṇī it is stated beginning with योनिः (*yoniḥ*). Its application is in the Mahāvrata sacrifice, for the mantras of the niṣkevalya recitation, as is indicated by the sentence of the Aitareya Āraṇyaka योनिष्ट इंद्र सदने अकारीत्येतस्य चतस्रः शस्त्वोत्तमामुपसंतन्वोत्तमया परिदधाति (*yoniṣṭa iṃdra sadane akārītyetasya catasraḥ śastvottamāmupasaṃtanvottamayā paridadhāti*) (Ai. Ā. 5-3-1).

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> योनि॑ष्ट इंद्र॒ सद॑ने अकारि॒ तमा नृभिः॑ पुरुहूत॒ प्र या॑हि ।  
> असो॒ यथा॑ नो अवि॒ता वृ॒धे च॒ ददो॒ वसू॑नि म॒मद॑श्च॒ सोम्यैः॑ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> योनिः । ते । इंद्र । सदने । अकारि । तं । आ । नृभिः । पुरुहूत । प्र । याहि ।  
> असः । यथा । नः । अविता । वृधे । च । ददः । वसूनि । ममदः । च । सोम्यैः ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र ते तव सदने सदनार्थं योनिः स्थानमकारि । हे पुरुहूत नृभिर्मरुद्भिः सार्धं तं योनिमा प्र याहि । सोऽस्माकं यथाविता रक्षिता सः भवसि सो-

### Printed p. 44

> स्माकं वृधे वर्धनाय चासः । तथा च वसूनि ददः । अस्मभ्यं देहि । सोम्यैरस्मदीयैर्ममदः । मादयस्व च ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: O Indra, a place (योनिः (*yoniḥ*)) has been made for your sitting. O much-invoked one, come to that place together with the leaders, the Maruts. As you are our protector, so be for our increase also; and thus give us wealth (वसूनि (*vasūni*)), and be exhilarated by our Soma offerings.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- ते (*te*)—your
- सदने (*sadane*)—for the seat
- योनिः (*yoniḥ*)—a fitting place
- अकारि (*akāri*)—has been made
- पुरुहूत (*puruhūta*)—O Indra, who are called by many
- नृभिः (*nṛbhiḥ*)—together with the Maruts
- तं (*taṃ*)—to that place of sacrifice
- आ प्र याहि (*ā pra yāhi*)—be gracious and come
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- यथा (*yathā*)—in the manner
- अविता (*avitā*)—in which you are the protector, in that manner
- असः (*asaḥ*)—be
- वृधे च (*vṛdhe ca*)—help also for our increase
- वसूनि च (*vasūni ca*)—wealth also
- ददः (*dadaḥ*)—give to us
- सोम्यैः (*somyaiḥ*)—with the Soma-juices offered by us
- ममदः (*mamadaḥ*)—be satisfied

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, a fitting place has been made for your seat. O Indra, you who are worthy of the invocation of many, come together with the Maruts to that place of sacrifice. Be with us, so as to be our protector. Be the cause of our increase also. Give us wealth. Be satisfied with the Soma offered by us.

##### English translation
A place has been prepared for you in the sacrificial chamber : proceed to it, invoked of many, along with the leaders (of rites, the Maruts), inasmuch as you are our protector, (promote our) prosperity : grant us riches : be exhilarated by the Soma.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> गृ॒भी॒तं ते॒ मन॑ इंद्र द्वि॒बर्हाः॑ सु॒तः सोमः॒ परि॑षिक्ता॒ मधू॑नि ।  
> विसृ॑ष्टधेना भरते सुवृ॒क्तिरि॒यमिंद्रं॒ जोहु॑वती मनी॒षा ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> गृभीतं । ते । मनः । इंद्र । द्विबर्हाः । सुतः । सोमः । परिसिक्ता । मधूनि ।  
> विसृष्टधेना । भरते । सुवृक्तिः । इयं । इंद्रं । जोहुवती । मनीषा ॥ २ ॥

### Printed p. 45

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र द्विबर्हाः । षष्ठ्यर्थे प्रथमा । द्विबर्हसो द्वयोः स्थानयोः परिवृढस्य ते तव मनो गृभीतमस्माभिः परिगृहीतं । सोमश्च सुतोऽभिषुतः ।
> मधूनि च परिसिक्ता पात्रेषु परिसिक्तानि । विसृष्टधेना विसृष्टजिह्वा मध्यमस्वरेणोच्चार्यमाणा सुवृक्तिः सुसमाप्तिरियं मनीषा स्तुतिरिंद्रं जोहुवती भृशमाह्वयंती भरते । संभ्रियते च ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: O Indra, द्विबर्हाः (*dvibarhāḥ*) stands in the nominative in the sense of the genitive: of you who are mighty in the two places (परिवृढस्य (*parivṛḍhasya*)), your mind has been grasped by us. The Soma is pressed, and the sweet juices have been poured round into the vessels. This praise (मनीषा (*manīṣā*)), uttered in the middle tone with loosened tongue (विसृष्टधेना (*visṛṣṭadhenā*)), well completed (सुवृक्तिः (*suvṛktiḥ*)), calls Indra again and again and is brought to him.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- द्विबर्हाः (*dvibarhāḥ*)—you who have grown great in both worlds
- ते (*te*)—your
- मनः (*manaḥ*)—mind
- गृभीतं (*gṛbhītaṃ*)—has been apprehended by us
- सोमः (*somaḥ*)—the Soma-juice
- सुतः (*sutaḥ*)—has been pressed
- मधूनि (*madhūni*)—the sweet juices also
- परिसिक्ता (*parisiktā*)—are filled into the vessels
- विसृष्टधेना (*visṛṣṭadhenā*)—recited without hindrance
- सुवृक्तिः (*suvṛktiḥ*)—of excellent composition
- इयं मनीषा (*iyaṃ manīṣā*)—this praise
- इंद्रं (*iṃdraṃ*)—Indra
- जोहुवती (*johuvatī*)—calling much
- भरते (*bharate*)—propitiates

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, your mind, grown great in both worlds, has been apprehended by us. The Soma-juice has been pressed. The sweet juices too are filled into the vessels. This praise, to be recited without hindrance and of excellent composition, calling Indra again and again, propitiates him.

##### English translation
Your purpose, Indra, is apprehended, you who are mighty in the two (worlds) : the Soma is effused : the sweet juices are poured (into the vessels) : this perfect praise uttered with loosened tongue propitiates Indra with repeated invocations:

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ नो॑ दि॒व आ पृ॑थि॒व्या ऋ॑जीषिन्नि॒दं ब॒र्हिः सो॑म॒पेया॑य  
> याहि ।  
> वहं॑तु त्वा॒ हर॑यो म॒द्र्यं॑चमांगू॒षमच्छा॑ त॒वसं॒ मदा॑य ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । नः । दिवः । आ । पृथिव्याः । ऋजीषिन् । इदं । बर्हिः । सोम्यपेयाय । याहि ।  
> वहंतु । त्वा । हरयः । मद्र्यंचं । आंगूषं । अच्छ । तवसं । मदाय ॥ ३ ॥

### Printed p. 46

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे ऋजीषिन्निंद्र नोऽस्माकमिदं बर्हिरिमं यज्ञं सोमपेयाय दिवः
> स्वर्गादा याहि । आगच्छ । पृथिव्या अंतरिक्षाच्च । आप पृथिवीत्यंतरिक्षनामसु पाठात् ।
> आ याहि । अपि च तवसं प्रवृद्धं बलवंतं वा मद्र्यंचं मदभिमुखं त्वा त्वामांगूषं स्तोत्रमच्छाभि मदाय मदार्थं हरयोऽश्वा वहंतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: O Indra Ṛjīṣin, come to this our sacred grass, this sacrifice, for the drinking of Soma, from heaven (दिवः (*divaḥ*)), the celestial world, and from the earth (पृथिव्या (*pṛthivyā*)), which here means the atmosphere, since पृथिवी (*pṛthivī*) is read among the names of the atmosphere. And let your horses (हरयः (*harayaḥ*)) bring you, who are vigorous or strong (तवसं (*tavasaṃ*)) and turned towards me (मद्र्यंचं (*madryaṃcaṃ*)), towards the praise (आंगूषं (*āṃgūṣaṃ*)), for your exhilaration.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ऋजीषिन् (*ṛjīṣin*)—O Indra
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- इदं बर्हिः (*idaṃ barhiḥ*)—to this place of sacrifice
- सोमपेयाय (*somapeyāya*)—for the drinking of Soma
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—from heaven
- आ याहि (*ā yāhi*)—be gracious and come
- पृथिव्याः (*pṛthivyāḥ*)—from the earth also
- आ (याहि) (*ā (yāhi)*)—be gracious and come
- तवसं (*tavasaṃ*)—you who are of great strength
- मद्र्यंचं (*madryaṃcaṃ*)—and turned towards me
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- आंगूषं अच्छ (*āṃgūṣaṃ accha*)—towards our praise
- मदाय (*madāya*)—for your delight
- हरयः (*harayaḥ*)—your horses
- वहंतु (*vahaṃtu*)—let them bring you

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, come to this our place of sacrifice from heaven, for the drinking of Soma. Come from the earth also. Let your horses bring you, who are of great strength and turned towards me, towards our praise, for your delight.

##### English translation
Come, Rijishin, from the sky, or from the firmament, to this sacred grass, to drink the Soma : let your horses bear you who are vigorous to my presence to (receive my) praise and for (your) exhilaration.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ नो॒ विश्वा॑भिरू॒तिभिः॑ स॒जोषा॒ ब्रह्म॑ जुषा॒णो ह॑र्यश्व याहि ।  
> वरी॑वृज॒त्स्थवि॑रेभिः सुशिप्रा॒स्मे दध॒द्वृष॑णं॒ शुष्म॑मिंद्र ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । नः । विश्वाभिः । ऊतिभिः । सजोषाः । ब्रह्म । जुषाणः । हरिअश्व । याहि ।  
> वरीवृजत् । स्थविरेभिः । सुशिप्र । अस्मे इति । दधत् । वृषणं । शुष्मं । इंद्र ॥ ४ ॥

### Printed p. 47

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे हर्यश्व हरिनामकाश्व सुशिप्र शोभनहनविंद्र विश्वाभिः सर्वाभिरूतिभी
> रक्षणाभिः सजोषाः संगतः स्थविरेभिर्वृद्धैर्मरुद्भिः सह वरीवृजच्छत्रून् भृशं
> हिंसन् अस्मे अस्मभ्यं वृषणं कामानां वर्षितारं शुष्मं बलवंतं पुत्रं दधत्
> प्रयच्छन् ब्रह्म स्तोत्रं जुषाणः सेवमानो नोऽस्माना याहि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: O Haryaśva, you whose horses are called Hari, O Suśipra, you of handsome jaws, Indra, come to us united with all protections (ऊतिभिः (*ūtibhiḥ*)), together with the aged Maruts (स्थविरेभिः (*sthavirebhiḥ*)), smiting the foes violently (वरीवृजत् (*varīvṛjat*)), granting us a strong son (शुष्मं (*śuṣmaṃ*)), a showerer of desires, and accepting our praise (ब्रह्म (*brahma*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- हर्यश्व (*haryaśva*)—you who have horses called Hari
- सुशिप्र (*suśipra*)—you of handsome cheeks
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- विश्वाभिः (*viśvābhiḥ*)—with all
- ऊतिभिः (*ūtibhiḥ*)—protections
- सजोषाः (*sajoṣāḥ*)—being united
- स्थविरेभिः (*sthavirebhiḥ*)—together with the aged Maruts
- वरीवृजत् (*varīvṛjat*)—and smiting the enemies
- अस्मे (*asme*)—to us
- वृषणं (*vṛṣaṇaṃ*)—a showerer of desires
- शुष्मं (*śuṣmaṃ*)—and mighty son
- दधत् (*dadhat*)—giving
- ब्रह्म (*brahma*)—the praise
- जुषाणः (*juṣāṇaḥ*)—accepting
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- आ याहि (*ā yāhi*)—be gracious and come

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, you who have the horses called Hari and handsome cheeks, come to us united with all protections, smiting the enemies together with the aged Maruts, giving us a son who is a showerer of desires and mighty, and accepting our praise.

##### English translation
Lord of bay steeds, propitiated by our praise, come to us with all your protections, sharing in satisfaction, handsome-chinned, with the ancient (Maruts), overthrowing repeatedly (your) foes, and granting us a strong and vigorous (son).

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ए॒ष स्तोमो॑ म॒ह उ॒ग्राय॒ वाहे॑ धु॒री॒३॒॑वात्यो॒ न वा॒जय॑न्नधायि ।  
> इंद्र॑ त्वा॒यम॒र्क ई॑ट्टे॒ वसू॑नां दि॒वी॑व॒ द्यामधि॑ नः॒ श्रोम॑तं धाः ॥ ५ ॥

### Printed p. 48

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> एषः । स्तोमः । महे । उग्राय । वाहे । धुरिइव । अत्यः । न । वाजयन् । अधायि ।  
> इंद्र । त्वा । अयं । अर्कः । ईट्टे । वसूनां । दिविइव । द्यां । अधि । नः । श्रोमतं ।  
> धाः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> महे महत उग्रायोद्गूर्णाय बलिने वाहे विश्वस्य वोढ्रे इंद्राय
> धुरीव रथस्यात्यो नाश्व इव वाजयन् बलं कुर्वन्नेष स्तोमोऽधायि । वृढायि । अथ प्रत्यक्षस्तुतिः । हे इंद्र यं त्वामयमर्कः स्तोता वसूनां वसूनि धनानीट्टे याचते स त्वं नोऽस्मासु द्यां दिवीव श्रोमतं श्रवणीयमन्नं पुत्रं वाधिधाः । अधिधेहि ।

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: This hymn of praise (स्तोमः (*stomaḥ*)) has been offered to mighty (महे (*mahe*)), fierce (उग्राय (*ugrāya*)), strong Indra, the bearer of the universe (वाहे (*vāhe*)), as a horse at the yoke of a chariot, making strength. Now a direct address: O Indra, you whom this praiser (अर्कः (*arkaḥ*)) asks for riches (वसूनां (*vasūnāṃ*)): bestow upon us famed food, or a son, such as the sky has in heaven (श्रोमतं (*śromataṃ*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- महे (*mahe*)—of great nature
- उग्राय (*ugrāya*)—of great strength
- वाहे (*vāhe*)—and bearer of the universe, addressing Indra
- धुरीव (*dhurīva*)—of a chariot
- अत्यः न (*atyaḥ na*)—like a horse
- वाजयन् (*vājayan*)—producing strength
- एषः स्तोमः (*eṣaḥ stomaḥ*)—this hymn
- अधायि (*adhāyi*)—has been offered
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- अयं अर्कः (*ayaṃ arkaḥ*)—this praiser
- वसूनां (*vasūnāṃ*)—riches
- ईट्टे (*īṭṭe*)—asks for
- दिवि (*divi*)—in the atmosphere
- द्यां इव (*dyāṃ iva*)—shining like the famed heaven
- श्रोमतं (*śromataṃ*)—and famed food
- नः (*naḥ*)—for us
- अधि धाः (*adhi dhāḥ*)—provide

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

This hymn of praise, producing strength like a horse of a chariot, has been offered to Indra, who is of great nature, of great strength, and the bearer of the whole universe. O Indra, this praiser asks for riches. Provide us with food that shines like the famed heaven in the atmosphere and is itself famed.

##### English translation
This invigorating praise, like a horse attached to a car, has been addressed to you who are mighty and fierce, the up-bearer (of the world) : this your adorer desires of you, Indra, riches : grant us sustenance famous as the sky in heaven.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ए॒वा न॑ इंद्र॒ वार्य॑स्य पूर्धि॒ प्र ते॑ म॒हीं सु॑म॒तिं वे॑विदामः ।  
> इषं॑ पिन्व म॒घव॑द्भ्यः सु॒वीरां॑ यू॒यं पा॑त स्व॒स्तिभिः॒ सदा॑ नः ॥

### Printed p. 49

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> एव । नः । इंद्र । वार्यस्य । पूर्धि । प्र । ते । महीं । सुमतिं । वेविदाम ।  
> इषं । पिन्व । मघवत्भ्यः । सुवीरां । यूयं । पात । स्वस्तिभिः । सदा । नः ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र नोऽस्मानेवैवं वार्यस्य । तृतीयार्थे षष्ठी । वरणीयेन
> धनेन पूर्धि । पूरय । ते तव महीं महतीं सुमतिमनुग्रहबुद्धिं वेविदाम ।
> भृशं लभेमहि । मघवद्भ्यो हविष्मद्भ्योऽस्मभ्यं सुवीरां शोभनपुत्रादियुतामिषमन्नं पिन्व । प्रयच्छेत्यर्थः । स्पष्टमन्यत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: O Indra, fill us in this manner with desirable wealth (वार्यस्य (*vāryasya*) stands in the genitive in the sense of the instrumental). May we richly obtain your great (महीं (*mahīṃ*)) favour, that is, your gracious disposition (सुमतिं (*sumatiṃ*)). To us who are rich in oblations (मघवद्भ्यः (*maghavadbhyaḥ*)) bestow food (इषं (*iṣaṃ*)) accompanied by excellent sons and the like (सुवीरां (*suvīrāṃ*)). The rest is clear.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- एव (*eva*)—in this very manner
- वार्यस्य (*vāryasya*)—with desirable wealth
- प्र पूर्धि (*pra pūrdhi*)—fill completely
- ते (*te*)—your
- महीं (*mahīṃ*)—great
- सुमतिं (*sumatiṃ*)—gracious disposition
- वेविदाम (*vevidāma*)—may we obtain abundantly
- मघवद्भ्यः (*maghavadbhyaḥ*)—to us who have food in the form of oblations
- सुवीरां (*suvīrāṃ*)—accompanied by heroic sons and the like
- इषं (*iṣaṃ*)—food
- पिन्व (*pinva*)—provide (O Indra and the other gods)
- यूयं (*yūyaṃ*)—you
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- सदा (*sadā*)—always
- स्वस्तिभिः (*svastibhiḥ*)—with blessings
- पात (*pāta*)—protect

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, fill us in this manner completely with the wealth we desire. May we obtain abundantly your great gracious disposition. To us, who have food in the form of oblations, provide food accompanied by heroic sons and the like. O Indra and the other gods, protect us always with blessings.

##### English translation
In this manner, Indra, satisfy us (with the gift) of desirable (wealth) : may we repeatedly experience your great favour : bestow upon us who are opulent (in offerings) food with male descendants : ever cherish us with blessings.

### Printed p. 50

## Sūkta 25 (Maṇḍala 7)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> आ ते मह इंद्रेति षडृचमष्टमं सूक्तं वसिष्ठस्यार्षं त्रैष्टुभमैंद्रं ।
> आ ते इत्यनुक्रांतं । महाव्रते निष्केवल्य एतमादीनि पंच सूक्तानि । सूत्रितं च ।
> आ ते मह इंद्रोत्युग्रेति पंच सूक्तानि । ऐ. आ. ५-२-२ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: The sūkta of six Ṛks beginning आ ते मह इंद्र (*ā te maha iṃdra*) is the eighth sūkta; its seer is Vasiṣṭha, its metre Triṣṭubh, its deity Indra. The Anukramaṇī records it with आ ते (*ā te*). In the Mahāvrata, at the niṣkevalya recitation, five sūktas beginning with this one are used. It is laid down by the sūtra आ ते मह इंद्रोत्युग्रेति पंच सूक्तानि (*ā te maha iṃdrotyugreti paṃca sūktāni*) (Ai. Ā. 5-2-2).

The sūkta आ ते मह इंद्र (*ā te maha iṃdra*) is the eighth sūkta in the second anuvāka. It has six Ṛks. Vasiṣṭha is the seer of this sūkta; Indra is the deity; the metre is Triṣṭubh. In the Anukramaṇī it is stated beginning with आ ते (*ā te*). Its application, from this sūkta onward for the five sūktas, is in the Mahāvrata sacrifice, for the mantras of the niṣkevalya recitation, as is indicated by the sentence of the Aitareya Āraṇyaka आ ते मह इंद्रोत्युग्रेति पंच सूक्तानि (*ā te maha iṃdrotyugreti paṃca sūktāni*) (Ai. Ā. 5-2-2).

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ ते॑ म॒ह इं॑द्रो॒त्यु॑ग्र॒ सम॑न्यवो॒ यत्स॒मरं॑त॒ सेनाः॑ ।  
> पता॑ति दि॒द्युन्नर्य॑स्य बा॒ह्वोर्मा ते॒ मनो॑ विष्व॒द्र्य१॒ं॑ग्विचा॑रीत् ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । ते । महः । इंद्र । ऊती । उग्र । समन्यवः । यत् । संअरंत । सेनाः ।  
> पताति । दिद्युत् । नर्यस्य । बाह्वोः । मा । ते । मनः । विष्वद्र्यक् । वि । चारीत् ॥ १ ॥

### Printed p. 51

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे उग्रोद्गूर्णौजस्विन्निंद्र यद्यदा समन्यवः । समनो मन्युरभिमानो
> यासां ताः समन्यवः । सेनाः समरंत युध्यंते संगच्छंते वा तदा नर्यस्य
> नरहितस्य महो महतस्ते तव बाह्वोः स्थिता दिद्युदायुधं । दिद्युदिति वज्रनामसु पाठात् । उत्कृत्यास्मद्वधाय आ पताति । आपततु । तव विष्वद्र्यग्विष्वग्गंतृ
> मनश्च मा वि चारीत् । अस्मास्वेव स्थिरं भवतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: O fierce Indra, you of uplifted might: when armies (सेनाः (*senāḥ*)) filled with equal fury (समन्यवः (*samanyavaḥ*), those whose pride is equal) clash in battle (समरंत (*samaraṃta*)), or meet, then the shining weapon (दिद्युत् (*didyut*), read among the names of the thunderbolt) that lies in the arms of you, great and kind to men (नर्यस्य (*naryasya*)), let it be raised and fall (आ पताति (*ā patāti*)) for the slaying of our foes. And let your mind, which goes everywhere (विष्वद्र्यक् (*viṣvadryak*)), not wander off (मा वि चारीत् (*mā vi cārīt*)); let it be fixed on us alone.
**NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- उग्र (*ugra*)—you of exceeding might
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- यत् (*yat*)—when
- समन्यवः (*samanyavaḥ*)—armies of pride like your own
- सेनाः (*senāḥ*)—armies
- समरंत (*samaraṃta*)—meet together in battle, then
- नर्यस्य (*naryasya*)—kind to men
- महः (*mahaḥ*)—and of great nature
- ते (*te*)—your
- बाह्वोः (*bāhvoḥ*)—in the arms
- विद्युत् (*vidyut*)—the shining weapon
- ऊती (*ūtī*)—for our protection
- आ पताति (*ā patāti*)—let it be hurled at the enemies
- विष्वद्र्यक् (*viṣvadryak*)—all-pervading
- ते मनः (*te manaḥ*)—your mind also
- मा वि चारीत् (*mā vi cārīt*)—let it not leave us and wander elsewhere

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra of exceeding might, when armies of pride like your own meet together in battle, let the shining weapon in your arms, you who are kind to men and of great nature, be hurled at the enemies for our protection. Let your all-pervading mind also not leave us and wander elsewhere.

##### English translation
Fierce Indra, when animated by like fierce armies, encounter them : let the bright (weapon) wielded by the arms of you who are mighty and the friend of man, descend for our protection : let not your all-pervading mind wander (away from us).

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> नि दु॒र्ग इं॑द्र श्नथिह्य॒मित्रान॒॑भि ये नो॒ मर्ता॑सो अ॒मंति॑ ।  
> आ॒रे तं शंसं॑ कृणुहि निनि॒त्सोरा नो॑ भर सं॒भर॑णं॒ वसू॑नां ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> नि । दुर्गे । इंद्र । श्नथिहि । अमित्रान् । अभि । ये । नः । मर्तासः । अमंति ।  
> आरे । तं । शंसं । कृणुहि । निनित्सोः । आ । नः । भर । संभरणं । वसूनां ॥ २ ॥

### Printed p. 52

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र दुर्गे युद्धे ये मर्तासो मर्ता अभ्यभिमुखाः संतो नोऽस्मानमंति अभिभवंति तानमित्रांछत्रून्नि श्नथिहि । निजहि । अपि च निनित्सोरस्मान्निंदितुमिच्छतो नरस्य तं शंसमाशंसनमारे दूरे कृणुहि । कुरु । अपि च नोऽस्मभ्यं
> वसूनां धनानां संभरणं समूहमा भर । आहर ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: O Indra, in the stronghold, that is, in battle (दुर्गे (*durge*)), smite down those enemies, the mortals who face us and overpower us (अमंति (*amaṃti*)). And put far away (आरे (*āre*)) the calumny (शंसं (*śaṃsaṃ*)) of the man who wishes to revile us (निनित्सोः (*ninitsoḥ*)). And bring to us a collection (संभरणं (*saṃbharaṇaṃ*)) of riches.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- दुर्गे (*durge*)—in hard battle
- ये मर्तासः (*ye martāsaḥ*)—those men who
- अभि (*abhi*)—rising against us
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- अमंति (*amaṃti*)—try to defeat
- अमित्रान् (*amitrān*)—such enemies
- नि श्नथिहि (*ni śnathihi*)—crush completely
- निनित्सोः (*ninitsoḥ*)—of the enemy who wishes to revile us
- तं शंसं (*taṃ śaṃsaṃ*)—that desire
- आरे (*āre*)—far away
- कृणुहि (*kṛṇuhi*)—make
- नः (*naḥ*)—for us
- वसूनां (*vasūnāṃ*)—of riches
- संभरणं (*saṃbharaṇaṃ*)—a collection
- आ भर (*ā bhara*)—bring and provide

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, in hard battle crush completely those enemies, the men who rise against us and try to defeat us. Make the desire of the enemy who wishes to revile us remain far away. Bring and provide for us a collection of riches.

##### English translation
Destroy, Indra, our adversaries in battle, those men who overpower us : remove far from us the calumny of the reviler : bring to us abundance of treasures.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> श॒तं ते॑ शिप्रिन्नू॒तयः॑ सु॒दासे॑ स॒हस्रं॒ शंसा॑ उ॒त रा॒तिर॑स्तु ।  
> ज॒हि वध॑र्व॒नुषो॒ मर्त्य॑स्या॒स्मे द्यु॒म्नमधि॒ रत्नं॑ च धेहि ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> शतं । ते । शिप्रिन् । ऊतयः । सुदासे । सहस्रं । शंसाः । उत । रातिः । अस्तु ।  
> जहि । वधः । वनुषः । मर्त्यस्य । अस्मे इति । द्युम्नं । अधि । रत्नं । च । धेहि ॥ ३ ॥

### Printed p. 53

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे शिप्रिन्नृजीषिन्निंद्र ते त्वदीयाः शतं बह्व्य ऊतयो रक्षाः सुदासे
> शोभनदानाय मह्यं संतु । सहस्रं शंसाः शंसनीयाः कामाश्च संतु । उतापि च
> रातिर्धनमस्तु । वनुषो हिंसकस्य मर्त्यस्य वधो हिंसासाधनमायुधं च जहि ।
> अपि चास्मे अध्यस्मासु द्युम्नं दीप्तिमदन्नं यशो वा रत्नं च धेहि । तथा च
> यास्कः । द्युम्नं द्योततेर्यशो वान्नं वा । अस्मे द्युम्नमधि रत्नं च धेहि ।
> अस्मासु द्युम्नं च रत्नं च धेहि । नि. ५-५ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: O Indra, Śipriṇ and Ṛjīṣin, may your hundred protections (ऊतयो (*ūtayo*)) be for me, the one who gives well (सुदासे (*sudāse*)). May there be a thousand praiseworthy desires (शंसाः (*śaṃsāḥ*)), and a gift of wealth (रातिः (*rātiḥ*)) also. Smite the weapon (वधः (*vadhaḥ*)), the instrument of killing, of the hostile mortal (वनुषः (*vanuṣaḥ*)). And bestow on us radiant (द्युम्नं (*dyumnaṃ*)) food or fame, and jewels. So Yāska says in the Nirukta (Ni. 5-5): द्युम्नं द्योततेर्यशो वान्नं वा (*dyumnaṃ dyotateryaśo vānnaṃ vā*), that is, dyumna comes from the root dyut and means fame or food; and "bestow on us dyumna and jewels" is explained by him as the same.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- शिप्रिन् (*śiprin*)—O Indra of handsome cheeks
- ते (*te*)—your
- शतं (*śataṃ*)—a hundred in number
- ऊतयः (*ūtayaḥ*)—protections
- सुदासे (*sudāse*)—let them be reserved for me, who give well
- शंसाः (*śaṃsāḥ*)—your blessings
- सहस्रं (*sahasraṃ*)—let them be in thousands
- उत (*uta*)—and
- रातिः (*rātiḥ*)—your gift of wealth
- अस्तु (*astu*)—let it be
- वनुषः (*vanuṣaḥ*)—who is hostile to us
- मर्त्यस्य (*martyasya*)—of the man
- वधः (*vadhaḥ*)—the weapon that kills
- जहि (*jahi*)—destroy
- अस्मे अधि (*asme adhi*)—to us (in us)
- द्युम्नं (*dyumnaṃ*)—radiant
- रत्नं च (*ratnaṃ ca*)—and a jewel
- धेहि (*dhehi*)—put in

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra of handsome cheeks, may your hundred protections be reserved for me, who give generously. May your blessings be in thousands upon me. And may your wealth be for me alone. Destroy the weapon that kills of the man who is hostile to us. Put into us a radiant jewel also.

##### English translation
May hundreds of your protections, handsome-chinned, be (secured) to the liberal donor (of oblations) : may thousands of blessings be bestowed (upon me) as well as wealth ; cast the fatal weapon on the mischievous mortal ; grant us food and wealth.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> त्वाव॑तो॒ हीं॑द्र॒ क्रत्वे॒ अस्मि॒ त्वाव॑तोऽवि॒तुः शू॑र रा॒तौ ।  
> विश्वेदहा॑नि तविषीव उग्र॒ ओकः॑ कृणुष्व हरिवो॒ न म॑र्धीः ॥

### Printed p. 54

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> त्वाऽवतः । हि । इंद्र । क्रत्वे । अस्मि । त्वाऽवतः । अवितुः । शूर । रातौ ।  
> विश्वा । इत् । अहानि । तविषीऽवः । उग्र । ओकः । कृणुष्व । हरिऽवः । न । मर्धीः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र त्वावतस्त्वत्सदृशस्य क्रत्वे कर्मणेऽस्मि । भवामि हि । हे शूर अवितुर्विश्वस्य रक्षितुस्त्वावतस्त्वत्सदृशस्य रातौ दाने चास्मीति शेषः । हे तविषीवो बलवन्नुग्र जस्विन्निंद्र विश्वेद्विश्वान्येवाहान्योकोऽस्माकं स्थानं कृणुष्व । कुरु । हे हरिवो हरिवन् न मर्धीः । अस्मान्न हिंस्याः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: O Indra, I am (अस्मि (*asmi*)) for the work (क्रत्वे (*kratve*)) of one like you (त्वावतः (*tvāvataḥ*)). And, O hero, I am for the gift (रातौ (*rātau*)) of one like you, the protector of the whole world, the verb being supplied. O mighty and fierce Indra, make for us a dwelling (ओकः (*okaḥ*)) for all our days. O lord of the Hari horses, do not harm us (न मर्धीः (*na mardhīḥ*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- त्वावतः (*tvāvataḥ*)—like yourself
- क्रत्वे (*kratve*)—in the work
- अस्मि हि (*asmi hi*)—I am indeed engaged
- शूर (*śūra*)—O valiant Indra
- अवितुः (*avituḥ*)—a protector
- त्वावतः (*tvāvataḥ*)—like yourself
- रातौ (*rātau*)—I am indeed worthy of the gift
- तविषीवः (*taviṣīvaḥ*)—O exceedingly mighty
- उग्र (*ugra*)—and fearfully powerful Indra
- विश्वेत् (*viśvet*)—of all
- अहानि (*ahāni*)—the days
- ओकः (*okaḥ*)—a dwelling for us
- कृणुष्व (*kṛṇuṣva*)—build
- हरिवः (*harivaḥ*)—O Indra, who have horses called Hari
- न मर्धीः (*na mardhīḥ*)—do not harm us

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, I am engaged in work like your own. O valiant Indra, I am worthy of a gift like yours, the protector's. O Indra, exceedingly mighty and fearfully powerful, build for us a permanent dwelling for as long as our life lasts. O Indra, who have the horses called Hari, do not harm us.
**NOTE**

##### English translation
I am (in dependence), Indra, upon the acts of such as you are, upon the liberality of a protector, here, such as you : vigorous and fierce Indra, give us a dwelling for all our days : lord of bay steeds, do us no harm.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> कुत्सा॑ ए॒ते हर्य॑श्वाय शू॒षमिंद्रे॒ सहो॑ दे॒वजू॑तमिया॒नाः ।  
> स॒त्रा कृ॑धि सु॒हना॑ शूर वृ॒त्रा व॒यं तरु॑त्राः सनुयाम॒ वाजं॑ ॥

### Printed p. 55

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> कुत्साः । एते । हरिऽअश्वाय । शूषं । इंद्रे । सहः । देवऽजूतं । इयानाः ।  
> सत्रा । कृधि । सुऽहना । शूर । वृत्रा । वयं । तरुत्राः । सनुयाम । वाजं ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> एते वयं वसिष्ठा हर्यश्वाय हरिनामकाश्वायेंद्राय शूषं सुखकरं स्तोत्रं कुत्साः कुर्वाणाः ॥ करोतेः कुत्सशब्दनिष्पत्तिः ॥ इंद्रे देवजूतं देवैः प्रेरितं सहो बलमियाना याचमानास्तरुत्रा दुर्गाणि तीर्णाः संतो वाजं बलं सनुयाम । लभेमहि । अपि च हे शूर वृत्रा वृत्राणि शत्रून् सुहना हंतुं सुकराणि सत्रा सर्वदा कृधि । कुरु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: We, the Vasiṣṭhas, make (कुत्साः (*kutsāḥ*), from the root करोतेः (*karoteḥ*), "to make") a pleasing hymn of praise (शूषं (*śūṣaṃ*)) for Indra, the lord of the horses called Hari (हर्यश्वाय (*haryaśvāya*)). Asking (इयानाः (*iyānāḥ*)) of Indra the strength (सहः (*sahaḥ*)) that the gods have impelled (देवजूतं (*devajūtaṃ*)), and having crossed over hard places (तरुत्राः (*tarutrāḥ*)), may we win (सनुयाम (*sanuyāma*)) strength (वाजं (*vājaṃ*)). And, O hero, make (कृधि (*kṛdhi*)) the enemies (वृत्रा (*vṛtrā*)) always (सत्रा (*satrā*)) easy to slay (सुहना (*suhanā*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- एते वयं (*ete vayaṃ*)—we, born in the family of Vasiṣṭha
- हर्यश्वाय (*haryaśvāya*)—for Indra, who has the horses called Hari
- शूषं (*śūṣaṃ*)—a hymn of praise that gives happiness
- कुत्साः (*kutsāḥ*)—making
- इंद्रे (*iṃdre*)—of Indra
- देवजूतं (*devajūtaṃ*)—impelled by the gods
- सहः (*sahaḥ*)—strength
- इयानाः (*iyānāḥ*)—asking
- तरुत्राः (*tarutrāḥ*)—crossing over hard situations
- वाजं (*vājaṃ*)—food
- सनुयाम (*sanuyāma*)—may we obtain
- शूर (*śūra*)—O Indra, the valiant
- वृत्रा (*vṛtrā*)—the enemies
- सुहना (*suhanā*)—the power to slay easily
- सत्रा (*satrā*)—always
- कृधि (*kṛdhi*)—bring about

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

We, born in the family of Vasiṣṭha, making a pleasing hymn of praise for Indra, who has the horses called Hari, and asking of Indra the strength impelled by the gods, and crossing over hard situations, may we obtain food. O valiant Indra, always bring about the power to slay the enemies easily.

##### English translation
These (Vasishthas) are offering grateful (adoration) to the lord of bay steeds, soliciting the strength assigned by the gods to Indra : make our enemies, Indra, easy to be overcome, and may we, safe from peril, enjoy abundance.

### Printed p. 56

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ए॒वा न॑ इंद्र॒ वार्य॑स्य पूर्धि॒ प्र ते॑ म॒हीं सु॑म॒तिं वे॑विदाम ।  
> इषं॑ पिन्व म॒घव॑द्भ्यः सु॒वीरां॑ यू॒यं पा॑त स्व॒स्तिभिः॒ सदा॑ नः ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> एव । नः । इंद्र । वार्यस्य । पूर्धि । प्र । ते । महीं । सुऽमतिं । वेविदाम ।  
> इषं । पिन्व । मघवत्ऽभ्यः । सुऽवीरां । यूयं । पात । स्वस्तिऽभिः । सदा । नः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र नोऽस्मानेवैवं वार्यस्य । तृतीयार्थे षष्ठी । वरणीयेन धनेन पूर्धि । पूरय । ते तव महीं महतीं सुमतिमनुग्रहबुद्धिं वेविदाम । भृशं लभेमहि । मघवद्भ्यो हविष्मद्भ्योऽस्मभ्यं सुवीरां शोभनपुत्रादियुतामिषमन्नं पिन्व । प्रयच्छेत्यर्थः । स्पष्टमन्यत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: O Indra, fill us in this manner (एव (*eva*)) with desirable wealth (वार्यस्य (*vāryasya*) stands in the genitive in the sense of the instrumental). May we richly obtain your great (महीं (*mahīṃ*)) favour, that is, your gracious disposition (सुमतिं (*sumatiṃ*)). To us who are rich in oblations (मघवद्भ्यः (*maghavadbhyaḥ*)) bestow food (इषं (*iṣaṃ*)) accompanied by excellent sons and the like (सुवीरां (*suvīrāṃ*)). The rest is clear.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- एव (*eva*)—in this very manner
- वार्यस्य (*vāryasya*)—with desired wealth
- प्र पूर्धि (*pra pūrdhi*)—fill completely (fill for us)
- ते (*te*)—your
- महीं (*mahīṃ*)—great
- सुमतिं (*sumatiṃ*)—gracious disposition
- वेविदाम (*vevidāma*)—may we obtain abundantly
- मघवद्भ्यः (*maghavadbhyaḥ*)—to us who have oblations
- सुवीरां (*suvīrāṃ*)—accompanied by heroic sons
- इषं (*iṣaṃ*)—food
- पिन्व (*pinva*)—provide (O Indra and the others)
- यूयं (*yūyaṃ*)—you
- सदा (*sadā*)—always
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- स्वस्तिभिः (*svastibhiḥ*)—with blessings
- पात (*pāta*)—protect

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, in this manner fill up for us the wealth that we desire. May we obtain abundantly your great gracious disposition. To us who have oblations, provide food accompanied by heroic sons. O Indra and the others, always protect us with blessings.

##### English translation
In this manner, Indra, satisfy us with the gift of desirable (wealth) : may we repeatedly experience your great favour : bestow upon us who are opulent in offerings food with male discendants : ever cherish us with blessings.

## Sūkta 26 (Maṇḍala 7)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> न सोम इंद्रमिति पंचर्चं नवमं सूक्तं वसिष्ठस्यार्षं त्रैष्टुभमैंद्रं तथा चानुक्रम्यते । न सोमः पंचेति । महाव्रत उक्थ्ये विनियोगः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: The sūkta of five Ṛks beginning न सोम इंद्रं (*na soma iṃdraṃ*) is the ninth sūkta; its seer is Vasiṣṭha, its metre Triṣṭubh, its deity Indra. The Anukramaṇī records it thus: न सोमः पंच (*na somaḥ paṃca*). Its application is in the Mahāvrata, at the ukthya.

The sūkta न सोम इंद्रं (*na soma iṃdraṃ*) is the ninth sūkta in the second anuvāka. It has five Ṛks. Vasiṣṭha is the seer of this sūkta; Indra is the deity; the metre is Triṣṭubh. In the Anukramaṇī it is stated as न सोमः पंचे (*na somaḥ paṃce*). It has already been said earlier that this sūkta is applied in the Mahāvrata sacrifice for the mantras of the niṣkevalya recitation.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> न सोम॒ इंद्र॒मसु॑तो ममाद॒ नाब्र॑ह्माणो म॒घवा॑नं सु॒तासः॑ ।  
> तस्मा॑ उ॒क्थं ज॑नये॒ यज्जुजो॑षन्नृ॒वन्नवी॑यः शृ॒णव॒द्यथा॑ नः ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> न । सोमः । इंद्रं । असुतः । ममाद । न । अब्रह्माणः । मघऽवानं । सुतासः ।  
> तस्मै । उक्थं । जनये । यत् । जुजोषत् । नृऽवत् । नवीयः । शृणवत् । यथा । नः

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> मघवानं धनवंतमिंद्रमसुतो नाभिषुतः सोमो न ममाद । न तर्पयति । सुतासोऽभिषुता अपि सोमा अब्रह्माणः स्तोत्रहीना न ममदुः । ममादेत्येतदाख्यातं बहुवचनांततया विपरिणतं सदत्र संबध्यते । अत एव त्रिष्टुपि सवनेषु
> 8

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: Soma that has not been pressed (असुतः (*asutaḥ*)) does not delight (ममाद (*mamāda*), that is, does not satisfy) Indra, the wealthy (मघवानं (*maghavānaṃ*)). Even the juices that have been pressed (सुतासः (*sutāsaḥ*)), if they lack prayer (अब्रह्माणः (*abrahmāṇaḥ*), devoid of praise), do not delight him. The verb ममाद (*mamāda*) is changed into the plural and construed with the latter clause as well. For this very reason, in the Triṣṭubh libations, it is only the Somas that have been praised with the Pavamāna hymns that are invoked.

### Printed p. 58

> पावमानैः स्तोत्रैः स्तुता एव सोमा हूयंते । अपि च नोऽस्मदीयं यदुक्थमिंद्रो जुजोषत् सेवेत यथा च नृवद्राजेवादरेण शृणवत् शृणुयात् तथा नवीयो नवतरमुक्थं शस्त्रं तस्मा इंद्राय जनये पठामीत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: And whatever hymn (उक्थं (*ukthaṃ*)) of ours Indra enjoys (जुजोषत् (*jujoṣat*)), and in the manner in which he listens (शृणवत् (*śṛṇavat*)) with respect like a king (नृवत् (*nṛvat*)), in that manner I compose and recite (जनये (*janaye*)) for that Indra a newer (नवीयः (*navīyaḥ*)) hymn, a recitation (शस्त्रं (*śastraṃ*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मघवानं (*maghavānaṃ*)—the wealthy
- इंद्रं (*iṃdraṃ*)—Indra
- असुतः (*asutaḥ*)—not pressed
- सोमः (*somaḥ*)—Soma
- न ममाद (*na mamāda*)—does not delight
- सुतासः (*sutāsaḥ*)—even if pressed
- अब्रह्माणः (*abrahmāṇaḥ*)—the juice that is without praise
- न (ममदुः) (*na (mamaduḥ)*)—does not delight (therefore)
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- यत् (*yat*)—which
- उक्थं (*ukthaṃ*)—uktha mantra
- जुजोषत् (*jujoṣat*)—he enjoys
- यथा (*yathā*)—in the manner in which
- नृवत् (*nṛvat*)—like a king, with respect
- शृणवत् (*śṛṇavat*)—he listens (in that same manner)
- नवीयः (*navīyaḥ*)—the newly composed recitation mantra
- तस्मै (*tasmai*)—to that
- जनये (*janaye*)—I compose and recite

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Soma that is offered without being pressed does not delight Indra, the wealthy. Even if pressed, the juice that is not joined with praises does not delight him. Therefore I compose and recite for Indra, in the same manner, such a newly composed recitation mantra as Indra will enjoy among our uktha mantras and will listen to with respect like a king.

##### English translation
The Soma uneffused delights not Indra : the effused juices please not Maghavan, unaccompanied by prayer : therefore I offer to him the praise that he may be pleased with : that, like a prince, he may listen to a novel (strain).

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उ॒क्थ उ॑क्थे॒ सोम॒ इंद्रं॑ ममाद नी॒थेनी॑थे म॒घवा॑नं सु॒तासः॑ ।  
> यदीं॑ स॒बाधः॑ पि॒तरं॒ न पु॒त्राः स॑मा॒नद॑क्षा॒ अव॑से॒ हवं॑ते ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उक्थेऽउक्थे । सोमः । इंद्रं । ममाद । नीथेऽनीथे । मघऽवानं । सुतासः ।  
> यत् । ईं । सऽबाधः । पितरं । न । पुत्राः । समानऽदक्षाः । अवसे । हवंते ॥ २ ॥

### Printed p. 59

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यद्यस्मादुक्थ उक्थे शस्त्रे शस्त्रे क्रियमाणे सोमो मघवानमिंद्रं ममाद मादयति नीथे नीथे स्तोत्रे स्तोत्रे क्रियमाणे सुतासोऽभिषुताः सोमा मादयंति तस्मादीमेनमिंद्रं सबाधः परस्परं मिलिताः समानदक्षाः समानोत्साहा ऋत्विजः पुत्राः पितरं न पितरमिवावसे तर्पणाय स्वरक्षणाय वा हवंते शस्त्रैः स्तोत्रैश्च स्तुवंति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: Because Soma delights (ममाद (*mamāda*)) Indra, the wealthy, as each recitation (उक्थ उक्थे (*uktha ukthe*)) is performed, and the pressed juices (सुतासः (*sutāsaḥ*)) delight him as each hymn (नीथे नीथे (*nīthe nīthe*)) is performed, therefore the priests, who are joined together (सबाधः (*sabādhaḥ*)) and of equal zeal (समानदक्षाः (*samānadakṣāḥ*)), invoke this Indra for his satisfaction or for their own protection (अवसे (*avase*)), as sons invoke a father, praising him with recitations and hymns.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यत् (*yat*)—for which reason
- उक्थेउक्थे (*uktheukthe*)—whenever each recitation mantra is recited
- सोमः (*somaḥ*)—Soma
- मघवानं (*maghavānaṃ*)—the wealthy
- इंद्रं (*iṃdraṃ*)—Indra
- ममाद (*mamāda*)—satisfies
- नीथेनीथे (*nīthenīthe*)—whenever each hymn is recited
- सुतासः (*sutāsaḥ*)—the pressed Soma juices satisfy him (for that very reason)
- ईं (*īṃ*)—this Indra
- सबाधः (*sabādhaḥ*)—those who have come together
- समानदक्षाः (*samānadakṣāḥ*)—those who have one and the same strength, the priests
- पुत्राः (*putrāḥ*)—sons
- पितरं न (*pitaraṃ na*)—as they call a father for protection
- अवसे (*avase*)—for protection
- हवंते (*havaṃte*)—they call

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

For the very reason that Soma satisfies Indra, the wealthy, whenever each recitation mantra is recited, and the pressed Somas satisfy him whenever each hymn is recited, the priests, who have come together and are of one and the same strength, call upon Indra for protection, as sons call upon a father for protection.

##### English translation
The Soma effused with reiterated prayer delights Indra : the effused juices, (offered) with repeated praise, (exhilarate) Maghavan : therofore (the priests), combining together and making like exertion, invoke Indra for protection, as sons (apply) to a father.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> च॒कार॒ ता कृ॒णव॑न्नू॒नम॒न्या यानि॑ ब्रु॒वंति॑ वे॒धसः॑ सु॒तेषु॑ ।  
> जनी॑इव॒ पति॒रेकः॑ समा॒नो नि म॑मृजे॒ पुर॒ इंद्रः॒ सु सर्वाः॑ ॥

### Printed p. 60

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> चकार । ता । कृणवत् । नूनं । अन्या । यानि । ब्रुवंति । वेधसः । सुतेषु ।  
> जनीऽइव । पतिः । एकः । समानः । नि । ममृजे । पुरः । इंद्रः । सु । सर्वाः ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वेधसः स्तोत्राणां विधातारः सुतेषु सोमेष्वभिषुतेषु यानि कर्माणि ब्रुवंति तानि वृत्रवधादीनि कर्माणींद्रः पूर्वस्मिन्काले चकार । नूनं संप्रत्यप्यन्यान्यानि कर्माणि कृणवत् । कुर्यात् । अपि च स इंद्रः सर्वाः पुरः शत्रुनगरीः समानः समवृत्तिरेकोऽसहायः पतिर्जनीरिव जायो इव सु नि ममृजे । सम्यक् शोधयेत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: The exploits (कर्माणि (*karmāṇi*)) such as the slaying of Vṛtra that the praisers (वेधसः (*vedhasaḥ*), the composers of hymns) speak of (ब्रुवंति (*bruvaṃti*)) at the pressing of the Soma (सुतेषु (*suteṣu*)), those Indra did (चकार (*cakāra*)) in former time; may he now also perform (कृणवत् (*kṛṇavat*)) other exploits. And may that Indra, alone (एकः (*ekaḥ*)), unaided, and of equal bearing (समानः (*samānaḥ*)), cleanse thoroughly (सु नि ममृजे (*su ni mamṛje*)) all the cities (पुरः (*puraḥ*)) of the enemy, as a husband (पतिः (*patiḥ*)) his wives (जनी इव (*janī iva*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वेधसः (*vedhasaḥ*)—the praisers and reciters
- सुतेषु (*suteṣu*)—at the times of pressing the Soma
- यानि (*yāni*)—whatever heroic deeds
- चकार (*cakāra*)—Indra did
- ब्रुवंति (*bruvaṃti*)—they praise, such
- ता अन्या (*tā anyā*)—other deeds of the same kind
- नूनं (*nūnaṃ*)—now (the same Indra)
- कृणवत् (*kṛṇavat*)—let him accomplish
- पतिः (*patiḥ*)—as a husband
- जनीः इव (*janīḥ iva*)—takes his wives under his own control
- इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—Indra
- सर्वाः पुरः (*sarvāḥ puraḥ*)—all the enemy cities
- समानः (*samānaḥ*)—in one and the same manner
- एकः (*ekaḥ*)—as the unequalled one
- सु नि ममृजे (*su ni mamṛje*)—let him defeat and take under his own control

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Whatever heroic deeds the praisers and reciters praise at the time of pressing the Soma as having been done by Indra, other deeds of the same kind let that same Indra now accomplish. As a husband takes all his wives together under his own control, let Indra, as the unequalled one, in one and the same manner, defeat all the enemy cities and take them under his own control.

##### English translation
Such exploits as his worshippers, when the Soma is effused, proclaim that he has achieved, let him now perform : may Indra, equal (to the task) and unaided, possess all the cities (of the Asuras) as a husband his wives.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ए॒वा तमा॑हुरु॒त शृ॑ण्व॒ इंद्र॒ एको॑ विभ॒क्ता त॒रणि॑र्म॒घानां॑ ।  
> मि॒थ॒स्तुर॑ ऊ॒तयो॒ यस्य॑ पू॒र्वीर॒स्मे भ॒द्राणि॑ सश्चत प्रि॒याणि॑ ॥

### Printed p. 61

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> एव । तं । आहुः । उत । शृण्वे । इंद्रः । एकः । विऽभक्ता । तरणिः । मघानां ।  
> मिथःऽतुरः । ऊतयः । यस्य । पूर्वीः । अस्मे इति । भद्राणि । सश्चत । प्रियाणि ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यस्येंद्रस्य मिथः परस्परं तुरो बाधमानाः संश्लिष्टा वा पूर्वीः पूर्व्यो बह्व्य ऊतयो रक्षाः संति तमेवैवमुक्तगुणमाहुः पूर्व ऋषयः । उतापि चाद्यापि स इंद्रो मघानां मंहनीयानां धनानां विभक्ता दातेति तरणिरापदस्तारयितेति शृण्वे । श्रूयते । तस्य च प्रसादादस्मे अस्मान् प्रियाणि भद्राणि कल्याणानि सश्चत । सेवंतां ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: The ancient seers (पूर्व ऋषयः (*pūrva ṛṣayaḥ*)) declare (आहुः (*āhuḥ*)) Indra to be of the qualities stated, he whose many (पूर्वीः (*pūrvīḥ*)) protections (ऊतयः (*ūtayaḥ*)) are mutually (मिथः (*mithaḥ*)) pressing upon the foe or joined together (तुरः (*turaḥ*)). And even today that Indra is heard of (शृण्वे (*śṛṇve*)) as the distributor (विभक्ता (*vibhaktā*)) of precious (मघानां (*maghānāṃ*)) riches and as the one who carries us across (तरणिः (*taraṇiḥ*)) misfortune. By his grace may pleasing (प्रियाणि (*priyāṇi*)) blessings (भद्राणि (*bhadrāṇi*)) attend (सश्चत (*saścata*), "let them serve") us.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यस्य (*yasya*)—of which Indra
- मिथस्तुरः (*mithasturaḥ*)—which are joined together and are destructive
- पूर्वीः (*pūrvīḥ*)—which are numerous
- ऊतयः (*ūtayaḥ*)—protections
- तं एव (*taṃ eva*)—that very Indra
- आहुः (*āhuḥ*)—the former seers have praised
- उत (*uta*)—and
- इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—that same Indra
- मघानां (*maghānāṃ*)—of precious riches
- विभक्ता (*vibhaktā*)—as the giver
- तरणिः (*taraṇiḥ*)—as the one who protects from calamities
- शृण्वे (*śṛṇve*)—is known as famous
- अस्मे (*asme*)—us
- प्रियाणि (*priyāṇi*)—pleasing
- भद्राणि (*bhadrāṇi*)—blessings
- सश्चते (*saścate*)—let them serve

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The former seers have praised that very Indra whose protections, destructive to the foe and numerous, are joined together. And that same Indra is known as famous, as the giver of precious riches and the protector from calamities. By his grace may pleasing blessings come to us.

##### English translation
Such have they proclaimed him : Indra is still celebrated as the distributor of riches, the transporter (beyond calamity), of when many and emulous are the protections : may acceptable benefits attend us.

### Printed p. 62

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ए॒वा वसि॑ष्ठ॒ इंद्र॑मू॒तये॒ नृन्कृ॑ष्टी॒नां वृ॑ष॒भं सु॒ते गृ॑णाति ।  
> स॒ह॒स्रिण॒ उप॑ नो माहि॒ वाजा॑न्यू॒यं पा॑त स्व॒स्तिभिः॒ सदा॑ नः॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> एव । वसिष्ठः । इंद्रं । ऊतये । नॄन् । कृष्टीनां । वृषभं । सुते । गृणाति ।  
> सहस्रिणः । उप । नः । माहि । वाजान् । यूयं । पात । स्वस्तिभिः । सदा । नः ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वसिष्ठो नृन् नृणां । षष्ठ्यर्थे द्वितीया । ऊतये रक्षायै कृष्टीनां प्रजानां वृषभं कामानां वर्षकारमिंद्रमेवं पूर्वोक्तप्रकारेण गृणाति । स्तौति । अथ प्रत्यक्षस्तुतिः । हे इंद्र नोऽस्मभ्यं सहस्रिणः सहस्रसंख्याकान्वाजानन्नान्युप माहि । प्रयॆच्छेत्यर्थः । स्पष्टमन्यत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: Vasiṣṭha praises (गृणाति (*gṛṇāti*)) in the manner stated above Indra, the showerer (वृषभं (*vṛṣabhaṃ*)) of desires, of the peoples (कृष्टीनां (*kṛṣṭīnāṃ*)), for the protection (ऊतये (*ūtaye*)) of men (नृन् (*nṛn*) stands in the accusative in the sense of the genitive). Then comes the direct address: O Indra, grant (उप माहि (*upa māhi*)) us food (वाजान् (*vājān*)) in thousands (सहस्रिणः (*sahasriṇaḥ*)). The rest is clear.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- नृन् (*nṛn*)—of men
- ऊतये (*ūtaye*)—for protection
- वसिष्ठः (*vasiṣṭhaḥ*)—Vasiṣṭha
- कृष्टीनां (*kṛṣṭīnāṃ*)—of all the peoples
- वृषभं (*vṛṣabhaṃ*)—the granter of wishes
- इंद्रं (*iṃdraṃ*)—Indra
- एव (*eva*)—in the manner stated before
- सुते (*sute*)—at the time of pressing the Soma
- गृणाति (*gṛṇāti*)—praises (O Indra)
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- सहस्रिणः (*sahasriṇaḥ*)—in thousands
- वाजान् (*vājān*)—foods
- उप माहि (*upa māhi*)—provide (O Indra and the others)
- यूयं (*yūyaṃ*)—you
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- सदा (*sadā*)—always
- स्वस्तिभिः (*svastibhiḥ*)—with blessings
- पात (*pāta*)—protect

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

For the protection of men, Vasiṣṭha praises at the time of the pressing of the Soma, in the manner described before, Indra, the granter of wishes to all the peoples. O Indra, give us foods in thousands. O Indra and the others, always protect us with blessings.

##### English translation
Thus does Vasishtha glorify Indra, the showerer (of benefits) upon the worshippers for the preservation of mankind : bestow upon us, (Indra), thousands of viands : ever cherish us with blessings.

## Sūkta 27 (Maṇḍala 7)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> इंद्रं नर इति पंचर्चं दशमं सूक्तं वसिष्ठस्यार्षं त्रैष्टुभमैंद्रं । इंद्रं नर इत्यनुक्रांतं ॥ महाव्रते निष्केवल्ये एतत्सूक्तमुक्तं तृतीयत्वेन ॥ ऐंद्रे पशौ वपापुरोडाशहविषामाद्यास्तिस्रः क्रमेणानुवाक्याः । सूत्रितं च । इंद्रं नरो नेमधिता हवंत इत्युरुं नो लोकमनु नेषि विद्वान् । आ. ३-७ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: The sūkta of five Ṛks beginning इंद्रं नर (*iṃdraṃ nara*) is the tenth sūkta; its seer is Vasiṣṭha, its metre Triṣṭubh, its deity Indra. The Anukramaṇī records it with इंद्रं नर (*iṃdraṃ nara*). In the Mahāvrata, at the niṣkevalya recitation, this sūkta is recited as the third. In the animal sacrifice to Indra, the first three Ṛks are in order the anuvākyā verses for the vapā, the puroḍāśa and the oblation. It is laid down by the sūtra इंद्रं नरो नेमधिता हवंत इत्युरुं नो लोकमनु नेषि विद्वान् (*iṃdraṃ naro nemadhitā havaṃta ityuruṃ no lokamanu neṣi vidvān*) (Ā. Sū. 3-7).

The sūkta इंद्रं नरः (*iṃdraṃ naraḥ*) is the tenth sūkta in the second anuvāka. It has five Ṛks. Vasiṣṭha is the seer of this sūkta; Indra is the deity; the metre is Triṣṭubh. In the Anukramaṇī it is stated as इंद्रं नरः (*iṃdraṃ naraḥ*). In the Mahāvrata sacrifice, when the mantras of the niṣkevalya recitation are recited, this sūkta is to be recited as the third sūkta. And in the animal oblation to Indra, when the vapā, the puroḍāśa and the oblation are offered, the first three Ṛks of this sūkta are to be recited in order as anuvākyā mantras, as is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra इंद्रं नरो नेमधिता हवंत इत्युरुं नो लोकमनु नेषि विद्वान् (*iṃdraṃ naro nemadhitā havaṃta ityuruṃ no lokamanu neṣi vidvān*) (Ā. Sū. 3-7).

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इंद्रं॒ नरो॑ ने॒मधि॑ता हवंते॒ यत्पार्या॑ यु॒नज॑ते॒ धिय॒स्ताः ।  
> शूरो॒ नृषा॑ता॒ शव॑सश्चका॒न आ गोम॑ति व्र॒जे भ॑जा॒ त्वं नः॑ ॥१॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इंद्रं । नरः । नेमऽधिता । हवंते । यत् । पार्याः । युनजते । धियः । ताः ।  
> शूरः । नृऽसाता । शवसः । चकानः । आ । गोऽमति । व्रजे । भज । त्वं । नः ॥ १ ॥

### Printed p. 64

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यद्यदा पार्यो युद्धभरणनिमित्तास्ताः प्रसिद्धा धियः कर्माणि युनजते प्रयुज्यंते तदा नरो नेतारो यमिंद्रं नेमधिता नेमधितौ संग्रामे हवंते ह्वयंति स त्वं शूरो नृषाता नृणां संभक्ता च शवसो बलस्य बलं चकानः कामयमानश्च सन् गोमति व्रजे गोष्ठे गोसमूहे नोऽस्माना भज । प्रापय ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: When (यत् (*yat*)) those well-known rites (धियः (*dhiyaḥ*)) undertaken for carrying through the battle are performed (युनजते (*yunajate*)), then the leaders (नरः (*naraḥ*)) call (हवंते (*havaṃte*)) upon Indra in the battle (नेमधिता (*nemadhitā*)). You, the hero (शूरः (*śūraḥ*)), the sharer among men (नृषाता (*nṛṣātā*)), desiring (चकानः (*cakānaḥ*)) strength (शवसः (*śavasaḥ*)), bring us (आ भज (*ā bhaja*)) to a pen (व्रजे (*vraje*)) filled with cattle (गोमति (*gomati*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यत् (*yat*)—when
- पार्याः (*pāryāḥ*)—performed for victory in battle
- ताः (*tāḥ*)—those well-known
- धियः (*dhiyaḥ*)—rites
- युनजते (*yunajate*)—are performed, then
- नरः (*naraḥ*)—the men who lead in battle
- इंद्रं (*iṃdraṃ*)—Indra
- नेमधिता (*nemadhitā*)—for victory in battle
- हवंते (*havaṃte*)—they pray to and call (O Indra)
- शूरः (*śūraḥ*)—you who are valiant
- नृषाता (*nṛṣātā*)—who help men
- शवसः (*śavasaḥ*)—strength
- चकानः (*cakānaḥ*)—and who desire
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- गोमति (*gomati*)—filled with cows
- व्रजे (*vraje*)—in the cattle-pen
- आ भज (*ā bhaja*)—cause to dwell

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The leaders among men call upon Indra in prayer in the rites such as sacrifices which they perform in the desire for victory in battle. O Indra, at such a time, you who desire the good of men, who are valiant and who desire strength, bring under our control also a cattle-pen filled with cows.

##### English translation
Men invoke Indra, in battle when those actions which lead to victory are performed : you are a hero, the benefactor of man, the desirer of prowess; place us in possession of pastures abounding with cattle.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> य इं॑द्र॒ शुष्मो॑ मघवन्ते॒ अस्ति॒ शिक्षा॒ सखि॑भ्यः पुरुहूत॒ नृभ्यः॑ ।  
> त्वं हि दृ॒ळ्हा म॑घव॒न्विचे॑ता॒ अपा॑ वृधि॒ परि॑वृतं॒ न राधः॑ ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यः । इंद्र । शुष्मः । मघवन् । ते । अस्ति । शिक्ष । सखिभ्यः । पुरुहूत । नृभ्यः ।  
> त्वं । हि । दृळ्हा । मघवन् । विचेताः । अप । वृधि । परिवृतं । न । राधः ॥ २ ॥

### Printed p. 65

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे पुरुहूत बहुभिराहूतेंद्र ते तव यः शुष्मो बलमस्ति तं शुष्मं सखिभ्यः स्तोतृभ्यो नृभ्यः शिक्ष । देहि । अपि च हे मघवन् हि यस्माद्दृळ्हा दृढानि पुरां द्वाराणि बिभेदिथेति शेषः । तस्मात्स त्वं विचेता विविक्तप्रज्ञः सन् परिवृतं तिरोहितं राधो धनमप वृधि । अस्मभ्यमपावृणु । नेति संप्रत्यर्थे ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: O Indra, invoked by many (पुरुहूत (*puruhūta*)), give (शिक्ष (*śikṣa*)) to your friends (सखिभ्यः (*sakhibhyaḥ*)), the praisers (नृभ्यः (*nṛbhyaḥ*)), that strength (शुष्मः (*śuṣmaḥ*)) which is yours. And, O wealthy one (मघवन् (*maghavan*)), since you have broken open the firm (दृळ्हा (*dṛḷhā*)) gates of the cities (the verb is to be supplied), therefore you, of clear understanding (विचेताः (*vicetāḥ*)), uncover (अप वृधि (*apa vṛdhi*)) for us the wealth (राधः (*rādhaḥ*)) that lies hidden (परिवृतं (*parivṛtaṃ*)). The word न (*na*) is in the sense of "now".

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- पुरुहूत (*puruhūta*)—called upon in prayer by many
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- ते (*te*)—your
- यः शुष्म (*yaḥ śuṣma*)—the strength which
- अस्ति (*asti*)—is famed, such strength
- सखिभ्यः (*sakhibhyaḥ*)—to your friends
- नृभ्यः (*nṛbhyaḥ*)—to the men who praise you
- शिक्ष (*śikṣa*)—give (and)
- मघवन् (*maghavan*)—O Indra, who are wealthy
- हि (*hi*)—for which reason
- दृळ्हा (*dṛḷhā*)—you have broken open the gates of the firm cities of the foe, for that reason
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- विचेताः (*vicetāḥ*)—are one who knows everything
- परिवृतं (*parivṛtaṃ*)—secretly hidden in the cities of the foe
- राधः (*rādhaḥ*)—wealth
- न (*na*)—now
- अप वृधि (*apa vṛdhi*)—bring to us

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, invoked by many, give the exceedingly great strength that you have to your friends, the men who praise you. O wealthy Indra, you are a discerning one who knows everything. Therefore break open the firm gates of the cities of the foe and now bring to us the wealth that is secretly hidden there.

##### English translation
Indra, you are the invoked of many, give to those men who are your friends that strength which, Maghavan, is yours; you, Maghavan, (have forced open) the firm (shut, gates of cities): discover, discriminator (of truth), the treasure now concealed,

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इंद्रो॒ राजा॒ जग॑तश्चर्षणी॒नामधि॒ क्षमि॒ विषु॑रूपं॒ यदस्ति॑ ।  
> ततो॑ ददाति दा॒शुषे॒ वसू॑नि॒ चोद॒द्राध॒ उप॑स्तुतश्चिद॒र्वाक् ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इंद्रः । राजा । जगतः । चर्षणीनां । अधि । क्षमि । विषुरूपं । यत् । अस्ति ।  
> ततः । ददाति । दाशुषे । वसूनि । चोदत् । राधः । उपस्तुतः । चित् । अर्वाक् ॥  
> 9

### Printed p. 66

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> स इंद्रो जगतो जंगमस्य पश्वादेर्यतो राजेश्वरो भवति चर्षणीनां मनुष्याणां च राजा भवति अधि क्षमि क्षमायां विषुरूपं नानारूपं यद्धनमस्ति तस्यापि राजा भवति ततो दाशुषे यजमानाय वसूनि धनानि ददाति । स इंद्रोऽस्माभिरुपस्तुत एव सन्राधो धनमर्वागस्मदभिमुखं चोदत् । प्रेरयतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: Since that Indra is lord (राजेश्वरो (*rājeśvaro*)) of the moving world (जगतो (*jagato*), of cattle and the like) and king (राजा (*rājā*)) of men (चर्षणीनां (*carṣaṇīnāṃ*)), and on the earth (अधि क्षमि (*adhi kṣami*)) is king also of whatever manifold (विषुरूपं (*viṣurūpaṃ*)) wealth there is, therefore he gives (ददाति (*dadāti*)) riches (वसूनि (*vasūni*)) to the sacrificer (दाशुषे (*dāśuṣe*)). May that Indra, who has been praised (उपस्तुत (*upastuta*)) by us, drive (चोदत् (*codat*)) wealth (राधः (*rādhaḥ*)) toward us.
**NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—Indra
- जगतः (*jagataḥ*)—of the world
- चर्षणीनां (*carṣaṇīnāṃ*)—of all men
- राजा (*rājā*)—is lord
- अधि क्षमि (*adhi kṣami*)—on the earth
- विषुरूपं (*viṣurūpaṃ*)—in many forms
- यत् (*yat*)—whatever wealth
- अस्ति (*asti*)—there is (he is the master of all that)
- ततः (*tataḥ*)—therefore
- दाशुषे (*dāśuṣe*)—to the sacrificer
- वसूनि (*vasūni*)—riches
- ददाति चित् (*dadāti cit*)—he gives
- उपस्तुतः (*upastutaḥ*)—that same Indra, praised by us
- राधः (*rādhaḥ*)—wealth
- अर्वाक् (*arvāk*)—toward us
- चोदत् (*codat*)—let him drive

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Indra is lord of the whole world and of all men. He is master of all the wealth of manifold kinds that there is on the earth. Therefore he gives riches to the sacrificer. May that same Indra, praised by us, bring wealth to us.

##### English translation
Indra is lord of the earth and of men : (his is) the various wealth that exists upon the earth: thence he gives riches to the donor (of oblations): may he, glorified by us, bestow upon us wealth.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> नू चि॑न्न॒ इंद्रो॑ म॒घवा॒ सहू॑ती दा॒नो वाजं॒ नि य॑मते न ऊ॒ती ।  
> अनू॑ना॒ यस्य॒ दक्षि॑णा पी॒पाय॑ वा॒मं नृभ्यो॑ अ॒भिवी॑ता॒ सखि॑भ्यः

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> नु । चित् । नः । इंद्रः । मघवा । सहूती । दानः । वाजं । नि । यमते । न । ऊती ।  
> अनूना । यस्य । दक्षिणा । पीपाय । वामं । नृभ्यः । अभिवीता । सखिभ्यः ॥ ४ ॥

### Printed p. 67

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> मघवा धनवान् दानो ददानः स इंद्रो नोऽस्माकं सहूती सहूत्या मरुद्भिः सहाह्वानेन वाजमन्नं नोऽस्मभ्यमूत्यै रक्षायै नू चित् क्षिप्रमेव नि यमते । प्रयच्छतु । यस्येंद्रस्यानूना संपूर्णाभिवीताभिप्राप्ता दक्षिणा दानं सखिभ्यः स्तोतृभ्यो नृभ्यो वामं वननीयं धनं पीपाय दोग्धि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: Let that Indra, the wealthy (मघवा (*maghavā*)) and giving (दानो (*dāno*)), who is invoked together (सहूती (*sahūtī*), that is, together with the Maruts), bestow upon us food (वाजमन्नं (*vājamannaṃ*)) quickly (नू चित् (*nū cit*)) for our protection; let him give it (नि यमते (*ni yamate*)). His gift (दक्षिणा (*dakṣiṇā*)) is full (अनूना (*anūnā*)) and has reached (अभिवीता (*abhivītā*)) all; it yields (पीपाय (*pīpāya*), "milks out") to his friends (सखिभ्यः (*sakhibhyaḥ*)), the praising men (नृभ्यः (*nṛbhyaḥ*)), desirable (वामं (*vāmaṃ*)) wealth.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यस्य (*yasya*)—of which Indra
- अनूना (*anūnā*)—which is complete
- अभिवीता (*abhivītā*)—and fit for our enjoyment
- दक्षिणा (*dakṣiṇā*)—the gift
- सखिभ्यः (*sakhibhyaḥ*)—to the praising
- नृभ्यः (*nṛbhyaḥ*)—men
- वामं (*vāmaṃ*)—the desired wealth
- पीपाय (*pīpāya*)—calls forth and gives (such)
- मघवा (*maghavā*)—wealthy
- दानः (*dānaḥ*)—and a liberal giver
- इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—Indra
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- ऊती (*ūtī*)—for protection
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- वाजं (*vājaṃ*)—food
- सहूती (*sahūtī*)—accompanied by the Maruts
- नू चित् (*nū cit*)—quickly
- नि यमते (*ni yamate*)—let him cause to be given

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Let Indra, the wealthy and liberal giver, whose complete gift, fit for our enjoyment, calls forth and gives the desired wealth to the praising men, accompanied by the Maruts, quickly cause food to be given to us for our protection.

##### English translation
May the affluent and liberal Indra, upon being invoked together (with the Maruts), quickly bestow food for our preservation, he whose unlimited, experienced liberality yields desirable (wealth) to those men (who are his) friends.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> नू इं॑द्र रा॒ये वरि॑वस्कृधी न॒ आ ते॒ मनो॑ ववृत्याम म॒घाय॑ ।  
> गोम॒दश्वा॑व॒द्रथ॑व॒द्व्यंतो॑ यू॒यं पा॑त स्व॒स्तिभिः॒ सदा॑ नः ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> नु । इंद्र । राये । वरिवः । कृधि । नः । आ । ते । मनः । ववृत्याम । मघाय ।  
> गोमत् । अश्वऽवत् । रथवत् । व्यंतः । यूयं । पात । स्वस्तिभिः । सदा । नः ॥

### Printed p. 68

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र नोऽस्मभ्यं राये धनप्राप्तये नु क्षिप्रं वरिवो धनं । वेदो वरिव इति धननामसु पाठात् । त्वं कृधि । धेहि । वयं ते तव मनो मघाय मंहनीयायै स्तुत्या आ ववृत्याम । आवर्तयाम । स्पष्टमन्यत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: O Indra, make wealth (वरिवो (*varivo*), read among the names of wealth) quickly for us, for the obtaining of riches (राये (*rāye*)). We turn (आ ववृत्याम (*ā vavṛtyāma*)) your mind toward us with an honourable (मघाय (*maghāya*)) praise. The rest is clear.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- नः (*naḥ*)—for us
- राये (*rāye*)—for the increase of wealth
- नु (*nu*)—quickly
- वरिवः (*varivaḥ*)—wealth
- कृधि (*kṛdhi*)—produce
- वयं (*vayaṃ*)—we
- ते (*te*)—your
- मनः (*manaḥ*)—mind
- मघाय (*maghāya*)—with honourable praise
- आ ववृत्याम (*ā vavṛtyāma*)—shall turn toward us
- गोमत् (*gomat*)—accompanied by cows
- अश्ववत् (*aśvavat*)—provided with horses
- रथवत् (*rathavat*)—provided with chariots
- व्यंतः (*vyaṃtaḥ*)—giving to us wealth of this kind (O Indra and the other gods)
- यूयं (*yūyaṃ*)—you
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- स्वस्तिभिः (*svastibhiḥ*)—with blessings
- सदा (*sadā*)—always
- पात (*pāta*)—protect

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, quickly produce wealth for us, for the increase of our riches. We shall turn your mind toward us with honourable praises. Giving us wealth accompanied by cows, provided with horses and provided with chariots, O Indra and the other gods, always protect us with blessings.

##### English translation
Indra, grant quickly wealth for our enrichment : may we attract your favour by our adoration : granting as (riches), comprising cattle, and horses, and chariots : ever cherish us with blessings.

## Sūkta 28 (Maṇḍala 7)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ब्रह्माण इंद्रेति पंचर्चमेकादशं सूक्तं वसिष्ठस्यार्षं त्रैष्टुभमैंद्रं । ब्रह्माण इत्यनुक्रांतं ॥ महाव्रते निष्केवल्ये पंचमत्वेनास्य सूक्तस्य विनियोग उक्तः ॥अश्वमेधेऽहनि प्रउगशस्त्रे आद्यस्तृच ऐंद्रः । आ. ८-१० । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa says: The sūkta of five Ṛks beginning ब्रह्माण इंद्र (*brahmāṇa iṃdra*) is the eleventh sūkta; its seer is Vasiṣṭha, its metre Triṣṭubh, its deity Indra. The Anukramaṇī records it with ब्रह्माण (*brahmāṇa*). In the Mahāvrata, at the niṣkevalya recitation, the use of this sūkta is stated as the fifth. In the Aśvamedha, on the day of the Praüga-śastra, the first three Ṛks form the Indra triplet: प्रउगशस्त्रे आद्यस्तृच ऐंद्रः (*praugaśastre ādyastṛca aiṃdraḥ*) (Ā. Sū. 8-10).

The sūkta ब्रह्माण इंद्र (*brahmāṇa iṃdra*) is the eleventh sūkta in the second anuvāka. It has five Ṛks. Vasiṣṭha is the seer of this sūkta; Indra is the deity; the metre is Triṣṭubh. In the Anukramaṇī it is stated as ब्रह्माणः (*brahmāṇaḥ*). Its application has been stated: at the Mahāvrata, at the time of the niṣkevalya recitation, this sūkta is to be recited as the fifth sūkta. And on the eighth day of the sacrifice, in the recitation of the Praüga-śastra mantras, the first three Ṛks of this sūkta are to be recited as the triplet addressed to Indra, as is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra (Ā. Sū. 8-10).

### Printed p. 69

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ब्रह्मा॑ण इं॒द्रोप॑ याहि वि॒द्वान॒र्वांच॑स्ते॒ हर॑यो संतु यु॒क्ताः ।  
> विश्वे॑ चि॒द्धि त्वा॑ वि॒हवं॑त॒ मर्ता॑ अ॒स्माक॒मिच्छृ॑णुहि विश्वमिन्व ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ब्रह्मा । नः । इंद्र । उप । याहि । विद्वान् । अर्वांचः । ते । हरयः । संतु । युक्ताः ।
